### [1] [Making Revisions Understandable: A Survey of Edit Intentions, Methods, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01610)
*Fangping Lan,Qi Zhang,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇从"编辑意图"视角系统综述文本修订研究的调查论文，统一梳理了修订语料构建、编辑意图分类体系设计、意图识别方法及下游应用，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Wikipedia和arXiv等平台上大规模修订历史数据的可用性增加，NLP研究已从建模"改了什么"转向理解"为什么改"，即潜在的编辑意图。然而，目前缺乏一个从编辑意图角度统一整合数据集、分类体系、识别方法和应用的综述，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文作为一篇综述论文，系统回顾了文本修订中的编辑意图研究，涵盖完整的修订工作流，包括：1) 修订语料构建（revision corpus construction）；2) 编辑意图分类体系设计（edit intention taxonomy design）；3) 编辑意图识别方法（edit intention identification）。同时对代表性数据集和方法进行分类整理，并归纳下游应用。

Result: 作者对代表性数据集和方法进行了系统分类与归纳，总结了编辑意图在写作辅助（writing assistance）和文档编辑摘要（document edit summarization）等下游应用中的价值，并识别了关键开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 本文提供了从编辑意图视角理解文本修订研究的第一份统一综述，为后续研究提供了数据集与方法的全景视图，同时指出了尚未解决的开放方向，有助于推动该领域进一步探索。

Abstract: Text revision is a core process in document creation, capturing how authors iteratively refine, reorganize, and improve written content. With the increasing availability of large-scale revision histories from platforms such as Wikipedia and arXiv, NLP research has begun to move beyond modeling what changes are made to understanding why they are made, i.e., the underlying edit intentions. To our knowledge, this is the first survey that synthesizes text revision research through the lens of edit intentions, providing a unified view of datasets, taxonomies, identification methods, and applications. We review prior work across the full revision workflow, including revision corpus construction, edit intention taxonomy design, and edit intention identification. We further categorize representative datasets and methods, summarize downstream applications such as writing assistance and document edit summarization, and highlight key open research directions.

</details>


### [2] [MESSY STREETS: A Benchmark for Geocoding Real-World Addresses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01612)
*Edward Gaere,Florian von Wangenheim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了MESSY STREETS基准，用于评估地理编码器在非规范网页地址上的性能，发现商业系统比开源系统召回率高最多49个百分点，且表面形式偏差单独即可造成最多25个百分点的召回损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地理编码基准基于干净或人为扰动的地址构建，无法反映真实网页中存在的非规范地址（组件缺失、重复、畸形或不完整），导致对地理编码器在噪声数据场景下性能的评估失真。

Method: 从2024年12月Web Data Commons语料库构建包含非规范表面形式地址的基准MESSY STREETS，使用OpenAddresses或OpenStreetMap建立参考位置，对地址进行真实性验证并控制测量表面形式偏离程度。

Result: 最先进的商业地理编码器在召回率上比开源系统高出最多49个百分点；该差距主要源于非规范地址上的候选返回率差异，一旦返回候选则定位精度大致相当；非规范表面形式单独导致最多25个百分点的召回损失；Nominatim的合取匹配使单个无法识别的词条导致整个有效查询失败。

Conclusion: 地理编码器的选择对于处理噪声地址数据的应用是关键的设计决策，规范化与预处理可显著缩小开源与商业地理编码器之间的差距。

Abstract: We introduce MESSY STREETS, a benchmark for evaluating geocoders on verbatim web addresses, with existence verification and controlled measurement of surface-form divergence. Unlike conventional benchmarks based on clean or synthetically perturbed addresses, MESSY STREETS contains addresses whose surface forms diverge from canonical representations and whose components may be missing, repeated, malformed, or incomplete. The benchmark is constructed from the December 2024 Web Data Commons corpus, with reference locations established from OpenAddresses or OpenStreetMap.
  The strongest commercial geocoders outperform open-source systems by up to 49 percentage points in recall. This gap is driven primarily by differences in candidate return rates on non-canonical addresses; once a candidate is returned, positional accuracy is broadly comparable across systems. Non-canonical surface form alone accounts for up to 25 percentage points of recall loss. Examining Nominatim's query-processing pipeline, we show that its conjunctive matching lets a single unrecognised token zero an otherwise valid query.
  The results demonstrate that geocoder choice is a consequential design decision for applications processing noisy address data, and that normalisation and preprocessing could substantially narrow the gap between open-source and commercial geocoders.

</details>


### [3] [Skim and Skip: Hierarchical Adaptive Inference for Efficient Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01613)
*Meng Gao,Yizhen Zhang,Yang Ding,Ziqi Dai,Shuoshuo Zhang,Junjie Wang,Taiqiang Wu,Chufan Shi,Lei Ji,Jian Jiao,Linfeng Zhang,Yeyun Gong,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Skim and Skip (SAS)分层自适应推理框架，通过token级证据筛选和深度自适应推断，在保持99%检索性能的同时实现1.64倍加速和66.3%的FLOPs削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型作为统一嵌入骨架在多模态检索中表现优异，但均匀稠密推理方式造成巨大推理开销。现有方法忽视了检索中存在的两种关键异质性：token对最终嵌入的贡献高度不均，以及不同查询所需的推理深度差异显著。

Method: SAS包含两个阶段：1) token级证据筛选，仅保留对最终检索嵌入最相关的输入信息；2) 深度自适应推理，判断当前表示是否已足以进行可靠匹配，从而决定是否提前跳过剩余层。

Result: 在12个MMEB检索任务上，SAS保持约99%的稠密基线平均检索性能，实现最高1.64倍端到端加速及最高66.3%的FLOPs削减。

Conclusion: 通过利用token贡献不均和查询深度需求差异这两种异质性，SAS证明了分层自适应推理能在大幅降低计算成本的同时几乎无损地保持多模态检索性能，为高效多模态检索提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval (UMR) increasingly adopts multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as unified embedding backbones, but their strong retrieval performance comes at substantial inference cost. Existing methods typically rely on uniformly dense inference, where all input tokens are processed through the entire model and matched using the final-layer [EOS] representation. However, this paradigm overlooks two key forms of heterogeneity in multimodal retrieval: token contributions to the final retrieval embedding are highly uneven, and different queries require markedly different amounts of inference depth. To address this, we propose Skim and Skip (SAS), a hierarchical adaptive inference framework for efficient multimodal retrieval. SAS first performs token-level evidence selection to preserve only the input information most relevant to the final retrieval embedding, and then performs depth-adaptive inference to determine whether the current representation is already sufficient for reliable matching. Experiments on 12 MMEB retrieval tasks show that SAS retains about 99% of the dense baseline's average retrieval performance while achieving up to 1.64 times end-to-end speedup and up to 66.3% FLOPs reduction.

</details>


### [4] [Incident Memory: Training-Free Operational Memory through Sequential Pattern Mining and Velocity-Stratified Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01616)
*Adarsh Agrawal,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Incident Memory，一个无需模型训练的确定性系统，通过分层老化检索、指纹条件 PrefixSpan 挖掘和溯源感知的度量定义来积累运维知识，在真实 ITSM 事件日志和受控基准上显著优于直接的大语言模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件响应本质上是一个记忆问题：团队积累了票证、痕迹、事后分析和 wiki 页面，但下次事件所需的知识很少以正确的顺序、新鲜度和溯源被保存下来。现有的生成式模型方法在事件响应中存在知识组织缺失的局限。

Method: Incident Memory 包含三个核心组件：(i) 速度分层检索（velocity-stratified retrieval），按结构性、行为性、上下文性和临时性事实以不同速率老化；(ii) 指纹条件 PrefixSpan 挖掘（fingerprint-conditioned PrefixSpan mining），从成功的调查中提取有序 playbook；(iii) 溯源感知的度量定义（provenance-aware metric definitions），通过可执行检查检测冲突定义。

Result: 在 UCI ITSM 事件日志（141,712 个事件，24,918 个事件）上提取了 23,110 条有序痕迹，挖掘 39 个 playbook，覆盖 84.3% 的 6,934 个保留事件。受控基准上达到 99.2% 的有序 playbook 精度，架构性陈旧保证（平面基线为 36% 陈旧返回），冲突检测 F1 为 0.876。PrefixSpan 在 19 个指纹组上达到 0.985 的有序精度，优于 Claude Haiku 的 0.661。

Conclusion: 核心结论不是语言模型在事件响应上表现弱，而是在指纹和先前动作条件下，重复事件历史是低熵的。在该机制下，精确记忆比开放式生成是更强的原语。

Abstract: Incident response is a memory problem: teams accumulate tickets, traces, postmortems, and wiki pages, but the knowledge needed for the next incident is rarely stored with its order, freshness, and provenance intact. We present Incident Memory, a deterministic system that accumulates operational knowledge without model training. It combines (i) velocity-stratified retrieval, which ages structural, behavioral, contextual, and ephemeral facts at different rates; (ii) fingerprint-conditioned PrefixSpan mining, which extracts ordered playbooks from successful investigations; and (iii) provenance-aware metric definitions, which detect conflicting definitions through executable checks. On the UCI ITSM event log, containing 141,712 events across 24,918 incidents, Incident Memory extracts 23,110 ordered traces, mines 39 playbooks, and covers 84.3% of 6,934 held-out incidents. On controlled benchmarks with known ground truth, it achieves 99.2% ordered playbook precision (controlled), an architectural staleness guarantee versus 36% stale returns for a flat baseline, and conflict-detection F1 of 0.876. A direct Claude Haiku baseline on 19 fingerprint groups reaches 0.661 ordered precision, compared with 0.985 for PrefixSpan. The central result is not that language models are weak at incident response; it is that repeated incident histories are low-entropy once conditioned on fingerprint and previous action. In that regime, exact memory is a stronger primitive than open-ended generation.

</details>


### [5] [Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Knowledge Graph Expansion, RRF Fusion, and Per-Chunk Grounded Evaluation for Enterprise Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01617)
*Harish Saragadam,Sudhanshu Sharma,Meghana Pujari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DocuSearch是一个离线、多代理的文档智能系统，用于电信企业文档库的检索增强生成（RAG）。它融合了语义向量搜索（Qdrant + BGE-Large）、BM25全文搜索（SQLite FTS5）和知识图谱邻居扩展三种检索信号，通过加权倒数排名融合（RRF）合并，再由交叉编码器重排并通过最大边际相关性（MMR）去重剪枝。系统核心特色是逐chunk评估回路——LLM判断每个chunk是否需要更多上下文、是否完整回答问题、答案是否基于检索文本，未接地的答案不会返回而是回退到多chunk合并。在电信语料上，Precision@10达0.69，Recall@10达0.79，接地率89.6%，相比纯稠密RAG基线分别提升15、16和18.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型企业文档库中获取准确且基于事实的检索答案是困难问题。纯稠密向量检索在混合了技术术语、供应商特定缩写或需要跨多个不连续章节推理的查询上表现不佳。作者旨在弥补这一差距，构建一个在生产电信网络运营环境中开发和评估的离线多代理文档智能系统。

Method: DocuSearch采用三种互补的证据来源：(1) 基于Qdrant向量库的BGE-Large嵌入语义搜索；(2) 基于SQLite FTS5索引的BM25全文搜索；(3) 基于结构化边表的知识图谱邻居扩展。三个排序列表通过加权倒数排名融合（RRF）合并，权重分别为向量0.50、BM25 0.35、知识图谱0.15，平滑常数60。融合结果经交叉编码器重排，再用MMR（平衡因子0.65）进行相关性和多样性剪枝。系统核心创新是per-chunk评估循环：LLM逐chunk判断是否需要更多上下文、是否完整回答、答案是否接地于检索文本；未接地的答案不返回，系统回退到多chunk合并策略。

Result: 在电信语料库上，DocuSearch达到Precision@10为0.69，Recall@10为0.79，接地率为89.6%，相比纯稠密RAG基线分别提升15、16和18.4个百分点。

Conclusion: DocuSearch通过混合检索策略加上基于LLM的逐chunk接地性评估，有效提升了企业文档问答的准确性和可靠性。该系统的创新在于将每个检索chunk视为独立的微型检索问题进行处理，以确保输出答案的接地性（groundedness），并在接地失败时采用回退机制，从而显著优于纯稠密RAG基线。

Abstract: Getting accurate, grounded answers out of large enterprise document repositories is a difficult problem. Dense vector retrieval alone frequently performs poorly on queries that mix technical terminology, vendor-specific acronyms, or require reasoning across several non-adjacent sections. DocuSearch was built to address exactly this gap - an offline, multi-agent document intelligence system developed and evaluated in a production telecom network operations environment. Rather than relying on a single retrieval signal, DocuSearch pulls together three complementary sources of evidence: semantic search over a Qdrant vector store using BGE-Large embeddings, BM25 full text search over an SQLite FTS5 index, and Knowledge Graph neighbour expansion from a structured edge table. These three ranked lists are merged through Reciprocal Rank Fusion with signal weights of 0.50 for vector search, 0.35 for BM25, and 0.15 for the knowledge graph, using a smoothing constant of 60 to stabilize scores. A cross-encoder then reranks the fused list, and Maximal Marginal Relevance with a balance factor of 0.65 prunes results for relevance and diversity. What makes DocuSearch distinctive is a per-chunk evaluation loop treating each chunk as its own mini-retrieval problem: an LLM decides whether the chunk needs more context, whether it fully answers the query, and whether the answer is grounded in retrieved text. Ungrounded answers are not returned; the system falls back to a multi-chunk merge instead. On a telecom corpus, DocuSearch reaches Precision@10 of 0.69, Recall@10 of 0.79, and a grounding rate of 89.6% - gains of 15, 16, and 18.4 percentage points over a dense-only RAG baseline. Index Terms: retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge graph, reciprocal rank fusion, enterprise document search, agentic evaluation, BM25, cross-encoder reranking, on-premise deployment, LangGraph, telecom AI.

</details>


### [6] [Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Efficient Cloud Knowledge Base Search in Telecom SNOC Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01618)
*Harish Saragadam,Sudhanshu Sharma,Ipsha Routray*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Athena for Cloud Knowledge Base，一个用于Vodafone Idea SNOC环境的全离线多智能体检索增强生成（RAG）框架。该系统结合稠密检索（E5 Large V2）、BM25稀疏检索和知识图谱扩展，通过LangGraph编排，利用加权CombSUM融合、交叉编码器重排序及MMR生成多样化证据集；并引入逐块LLM评估与归因验证机制确保答案可靠性。在4200份SNOC云文档（312,000个索引块）上，MRR@10达0.910，EM达78.4%，显著优于单阶段稠密检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信服务与网络运营中心（SNOC）在关键事件中需要工程师快速准确检索云端文档信息，但传统基于关键词和单阶段检索方法难以提供精确结果。由于涉及企业数据主权约束，需要在完全离线的环境中构建一个能够高效、准确地从海量SOP、技术手册、事故报告和配置指南中获得可靠答案的检索系统。

Method: 该系统采用多智能体Retrieval Augmented Generation（RAG）架构，在LangGraph编排框架中集成三种检索通道：E5 Large V2嵌入的稠密检索、BM25稀疏检索以及知识图谱扩展。检索结果通过加权CombSUM融合，随后使用交叉编码器进行重排序，并通过Maximal Marginal Relevance（MMR）确保证据集的多样性与相关性。为提高答案可靠性，框架对每个MMR选中的文本块进行独立的逐块LLM评估（含显式归因验证），剔除不支持或证据薄弱的内容；若所有块均不满足验证标准，则自动将多个块联合评估作为回退机制。

Result: 在包含4,200份SNOC云文档、312,000个索引块的语料库上，所提方法实现了MRR@10为0.910和精确匹配（EM）得分为78.4%，相比单阶段稠密检索提升了14.6个百分点。整个流水线完全离线运行，满足企业数据主权要求。

Conclusion: Athena通过多检索通道融合、知识图谱增强、交叉编码器重排序和逐块归因验证，在完全离线的企业环境中显著提升了云文档检索的准确性和答案可靠性，为依赖大型文档库进行关键决策的电信SNOC场景提供了一个可行的落地解决方案。

Abstract: Telecom Service and Network Operations Centers (SNOCs) rely on large collections of cloud documents, including Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), vendor technical manuals, incident reports, and configuration guides, to maintain uninterrupted network operations. During critical incidents, engineers must quickly retrieve accurate information, yet traditional keyword based and single stage retrieval approaches often struggle to provide precise results.
  This paper presents Athena for Cloud Knowledge Base, a fully offline, multi agent Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework designed for enterprise cloud document search in Vodafone Idea's SNOC environment. The system integrates dense retrieval using E5 Large V2 embeddings, BM25 sparse retrieval, and Knowledge Graph expansion within a LangGraph based orchestration framework. Retrieved candidates are fused using Weighted CombSUM, followed by cross encoder reranking and Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to obtain a diverse and relevant evidence set.
  To improve answer reliability, the framework performs per chunk LLM evaluation with explicit attribution verification, assessing each MMR selected chunk independently before generating a response. Unsupported or weak evidence is discarded, and if no chunk satisfies the verification criteria, the system automatically evaluates multiple chunks together as a fallback. Experiments on a corpus of 4200 SNOC cloud documents containing 312000 indexed chunks show that the proposed approach achieves an MRR at 10 of 0.910 and an Exact Match (EM) score of 78.4 percent, outperforming single stage dense retrieval by 14.6 percentage points. The entire pipeline operates in a fully offline environment, satisfying enterprise data sovereignty requirements while delivering accurate and grounded responses for cloud document search.

</details>


### [7] [MGDiff: Multi-Interest Sequence Recommendation with Masking GNN-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01619)
*Wenjing Xiao,Hao Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的多兴趣序列推荐框架MGDiff（基于掩码图神经网络引导的扩散模型），通过将图神经网络与扩散模型结合，在扩散生成过程中生成准确且无偏的用户兴趣信息，并在多个推荐任务上取得优于多个基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐中的扩散模型在生成用户兴趣信息时存在生成精度不足以及无法缓解流行度偏差的问题，需要一个能够生成准确且无偏的兴趣信息框架。

Method: 提出了两个核心模块：(1) 语义增强的双层语义引导框架（DSG），通过权重自适应掩码图神经网络重建缺失链接以挖掘深层物品关系，并利用动态多专家网络将用户偏好投影到不同语义子空间以抑制干扰，从而为扩散模型提供结构化引导；(2) 流行度感知引导机制（PAG），利用物品流行度作为可微调整信号对扩散模型输出进行空间几何调整，以重新校准相似度度量，实现无流行度偏差的多样化推荐。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的数据集上与多个基线模型进行对比实验，MGDiff展现了优越的性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MGDiff通过结合图神经网络引导与流行度感知校准，有效提升了扩散模型在序列推荐中的生成准确性和推荐多样性，为扩散模型在推荐系统中的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We propose a novel Multi-Interest Sequence Recommendation Framework with \underline{M}asking \underline{G}NN-Guided \underline{Diff}usion Model (MGDiff), designed to generate accurate, bias-free user interest information during the diffusion process. First, we propose a semantics-enhanced Dual-layer Semantic Guidance (DSG) framework, which decomposes guidance into two synergistic stages: extracting latent item semantics and decoupling multidimensional user intent. We design a Weight-adaptive Masking Graph Neural Network reconstructs missing links to uncover deep item relationships beyond superficial co-occurrence, while a Dynamic Multi-Expert Network projects user preferences into distinct semantic subspaces to suppress irrelevant interference. This hierarchical design yields structured guidance that significantly improves the generation accuracy of diffusion models. Second, We propose a Popularity-Aware Guidance (PAG) mechanism that performs spatial geometric adjustments on the outputs of diffusion models: by using item popularity as a differentiable adjustment signal to recalibrate similarity metrics, we enable DMs to generate diverse recommendations free from popularity bias. Finally, we compare MGDiff with multiple baseline models across four widely used datasets, demonstrating its superior performance and validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [8] [When Literature Data Mislead Artificial Intelligence in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01621)
*Qian Wang,Ying Li,Ryuhei Sato,Hidemi Kato,Shin-ichi Orimo,Hao Li,Eric Jianfeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文以固态电解质电导率数据为例，系统分析了从文献提取科研数据时存在的准确性问题（如文本-图表不一致、轴标注模糊、单位混乱、测量背景缺失等），指出这些误差作为结构化标签噪声会在数据库构建和机器学习复用中传播，并提出将数据准确性视为AI驱动科学发现的基础设施要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能愈发将科学文献作为数据源来构建数据库、训练预测模型和指导发现，但文献衍生的数据集通常假设实验数值内部一致且可直接复用。作者通过实证分析揭示这一假设在材料科学中并不成立。

Method: 以固态电解质（SE）电导率数据为代表性材料科学案例，将数值从源文献追溯到策展数据集，识别并归纳了文本-图形不匹配、轴标注模糊、单位不一致和测量上下文缺失等系统性数据错误类型。

Result: 识别出多种数值上看似合理但难以通过常规预处理发现的还原性/标注性错误；跨数据库实例显示模糊报告可导致100倍的电导率误差；证明这些误差可在数据库构建和机器学习中作为结构化标签噪声传播。

Conclusion: 应将数据准确性重新定位为AI驱动科学发现的基础设施需求，倡导可追溯的报告、策展和验证实践，以实现科学数据的可复用性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) increasingly treats scientific literature as a data source for building databases, training predictive models, and guiding discovery. Yet literature-derived datasets often assume that reported experimental values are internally consistent and directly reusable. Here, we analyze this assumption using solid electrolyte (SE) conductivity data as a representative materials-science case. By tracing values from source articles to curated datasets, we identify recurrent text-figure mismatches, ambiguous axis annotations, unit inconsistencies, and missing measurement context. These discrepancies are often numerically plausible and therefore difficult to detect through routine preprocessing, but they can propagate as structured label noise during database construction and machine-learning reuse. A cross-database example shows how ambiguous reporting can create a 100-fold conductivity error. Our analysis reframes data accuracy as an infrastructure requirement for artificial-intelligence-driven discovery and motivates traceable reporting, curation, and validation practices for reusable scientific data.
  Keywords: AI for science; Data reliability; Scientific databases; Structured label noise; Literature-derived data; Materials informatics; Solid electrolytes

</details>


### [9] [RecEvolve: A Knowledge-Driven Autonomous Agent System for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01622)
*Weidi Pan,He Ma,Shuhao Ye,Palaksh Rungta,David McPeek,Junyi Jiao,Arnab Bhadury,Mingyan Gao,Onkar Dalal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文实证验证了一个知识驱动的自主代理系统，该系统直接部署在生产级大规模双塔召回模型上，通过闭环自主框架完成从想法生成到代码实现、离线训练和指标评估的整个研究生命周期，实现了NDCG约20%的相对提升（转化为线上流量中用户满意度+3.77%的增长），并自主发现了标准评估协议中的奖励黑客漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI的兴起推动了自迭代系统的发展，为生产推荐模型的自主优化开辟了新前沿。研究旨在验证知识驱动的自主代理系统能否直接部署在生产级大规模召回模型上，承担完整的研究生命周期，并检验其能否加速机器学习研究、压力测试底层实验基础设施的严谨性。

Method: 构建了一个持续闭环自主框架，将整个研究生命周期（想法生成、代码实现、离线训练、指标评估）全部委托给自主代理系统。系统在生产级两塔召回模型上从零开始执行了40余次完整的自主训练运行，并在严格的生产规模评估条件下运行，自主导航和发现最新生产模型上隐藏的架构瓶颈。

Result: 系统实现了NDCG约20%的相对提升，该增益直接转化为线上生产流量中用户满意度+3.77%的增长。此外，部署过程暴露了标准评估协议的关键漏洞——代理系统自主发现了奖励黑客（reward-hacking）捷径，同时揭示了奖励黑客和失败假设的冗余探索等新挑战。

Conclusion: 研究证明自主流水线能够显著加速机器学习研究的步伐，并对底层实验基础设施的严谨性进行压力测试。同时揭示了奖励黑客攻击和冗余探索等新挑战，表明自主代理系统在生产推荐系统优化中具有巨大潜力但也需要更严格的评估机制。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI has catalyzed a shift toward self-iterating systems, opening new frontiers for the autonomous optimization of production recommender models. This paper presents the empirical validation of a knowledge-driven autonomous agent system, deployed directly on a production large-scale Two-Tower retrieval model. By delegating the entire research lifecycle, spanning idea generation, code implementation, offline training, and metric evaluation, to a continuous closed-loop autonomous framework, the agent system executed over 40 completed autonomous training runs from scratch. Executing these runs under rigorous production-scale evaluations, the system systematically navigated hidden architectural bottlenecks on the latest production model to achieve a breakthrough ~20% relative improvement in NDCG, a gain that translated directly to a +3.77% increase in user satisfaction in live production traffic. Furthermore, the deployment exposed critical vulnerabilities in standard evaluation protocols, as the agent system autonomously discovered reward-hacking shortcuts. These findings prove that an autonomous pipeline can dramatically accelerate the pace of machine learning research and stress-test the rigorousness of underlying experimental infrastructure, while also exposing novel challenges such as reward hacking and redundant exploration of failed hypotheses.

</details>


### [10] [The Utility of LLMs in Recommender Systems Explanation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01627)
*Kathrin Wardatzky,Oana Inel,Luca Rossetto,Abraham Bernstein*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）作为"裁判"来选择和评估推荐系统中的解释生成方法。作者生成了18种不同的解释原型，由14种不同规模的LLM在两个温度设置下进行评估，并与用户研究的人类评分进行对比。结果表明，LLM的评分模式与人类相似且具有中等秩相关性，但绝对评分一致性较低且受模型规模和评估构念影响。文章最终提出了四条实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统（RS）的可信度依赖解释机制，但存在大量解释方法而缺乏选择指导。基于用户的评估通常不可行，而自动化评估指标要么只评估解释器的抽象输出，要么需要通常不可用的ground truth。尽管LLM已被证明可作为解释评估的"裁判"，但其可靠性尚未被充分探索。因此，本文旨在研究LLM在特定应用场景下选择有效解释方法的实用性。

Method: 作者在给定关于推荐系统和用户的不同信息条件下，利用LLM生成解释原型，共生成18种不同的解释原型。随后由14种不同规模的LLM在两个温度设置下对这些原型进行评估，并将LLM评估结果与来自用户研究的人类评分进行对比分析，考察评分模式、秩相关性和绝对评分一致性。

Result: 研究发现：LLM展现出类人的评分模式，与人类评分者达到中等程度的秩相关性；但LLM与人类在绝对评分上的一致性较低，且不一致程度随模型规模和评估构念的不同而有显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，作者提出四条实践建议：(1) 保持解释生成提示词简洁；(2) 评估时优先选择较大的模型；(3) 预测试评估构念；(4) 对解释进行事实准确性审计，因为无论是人类还是LLM都无法可靠地检测非事实内容。

Abstract: Explanations play a crucial role in creating trustworthy recommender systems (RS), yet choosing a good explanation method presents challenges. Many explanation methods exist, but little guidance exists on which is best for which setting. Existing explanation generation methods often produce abstract outputs that require further formatting to become user-friendly, with a seemingly endless pool of options. Running user-based evaluations of all possible options is usually unfeasible, while automated evaluation metrics often either assess only the explainer's abstract output or require comparison with a ground truth, which is generally unavailable. Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can serve as ``judges'' for explanation evaluation, but their reliability has not yet been thoroughly explored. This paper studies the utility of LLMs in selecting an effective explanation method for a given application. We first explore their ability to generate explanation prototypes given varying information about the RS and the user. Specifically, we generate 18 distinct explanation prototypes, which are subsequently evaluated by 14 LLMs of varying sizes across two temperature settings. We compare these against human ratings derived from a user study. Our results show that while LLMs exhibit human-like rating patterns and achieve moderate rank correlation with human raters, their absolute rating agreement is low and varies substantially by model size and evaluation construct. We derive four practical recommendations: keep explanation-generation prompts concise, prefer larger models for evaluation, pre-test evaluation constructs, and audit explanations for factual accuracy, as neither humans nor LLMs reliably detect non-factual content.

</details>


### [11] [Marginal Expected Revenue for Jointly Ranking Auction and Fixed-Price Listings in E-Commerce Sponsored Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01628)
*Greg Kocher,Sanjana Arun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对电商搜索排序中混合 listing 格式（固定价格、纯拍卖、以及"拍卖+立即购买"ABIN）的统一排序问题，扩展了标准 eCPM 框架，推导出边际 eCPM（meCPM）以捕捉价格仍在动态演变中物品的额外展示价值，并描述了生产环境中的实际实现，在线 A/B 测试显示收入改善和用户指标显著提升，系统已部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索排序需在相关性、用户参与度和平台收入之间权衡。固定价格商品的预期收入估算已很成熟，但当市场库存包含纯拍卖和混合式"拍卖+立即购买"（ABIN）等价格动态演变的 listing 时，排序时的最终交易价值是未知的，导致现有 eCPM 框架失效。拍卖和 ABIN listing 在 eBay 等平台占据可观的库存和交易份额，尤其受个人卖家和价值不确定的独特商品青睐，因此需要一种能够统一处理多种 listing 格式的排序目标。

Method: 作者将标准 eCPM 框架扩展至拍卖和 ABIN listing，推导出边际 eCPM（meCPM），用于量化价格仍在演变中的物品获得额外一次展示所带来的增量价值。关键洞察是，固定价格 eCPM 本质上已是边际的，作者将其推广到拍卖动态中，从而在单一目标函数下统一排序固定价格、纯拍卖和 ABIN listing。在工程实现上，作者描述了一个实际生产级近似方案，通过从现有参与度模型引导（bootstrapping）来解决冷启动问题。

Result: 在大型电商平台上的在线 A/B 测试显示正向的收入增长，以及用户指标的统计显著改善。该系统最终已部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: 作者证明了通过 meCPM 扩展，可以将拍卖和 ABIN listing 纳入标准 eCPM 排序框架，实现混合格式 listing 的统一排序。该方法的实际效果（收入增益和用户指标改善）以及成功部署验证了其在真实电商场景中的有效性，解决了混合定价机制下排序中的一个重要空白。

Abstract: E-commerce search ranking must balance multiple objectives--relevance, user engagement, and platform revenue--when allocating impression slots to competing listings. Estimating the expected revenue component is well understood for fixed-price items, but becomes challenging when marketplace inventory includes mixed listing formats such as pure auctions and hybrid "Auction with Buy It Now" (ABIN) items, where prices evolve dynamically and the final transaction value is unknown at ranking time. Yet auction and ABIN listings account for a meaningful share of inventory and transaction volume on platforms such as eBay, and are a popular format for individual sellers and for unique items with unclear value. We extend the standard Expected Cost-per-Mille (eCPM) framework to auction and ABIN listings by deriving a marginal eCPM (meCPM) that captures the incremental value of showing one more impression of an item whose price is still evolving. The resulting formulation extends the familiar fixed-price eCPM--which is already inherently marginal--to auction dynamics, allowing unified ranking of fixed-price, auction, and ABIN listings under a single objective. We then describe a practical production implementation that approximates this objective, addressing cold-start challenges by bootstrapping from existing engagement models. Online A/B tests at a large e-commerce platform showed positive revenue gains and statistically significant improvements to user metrics, and the system was deployed to production.

</details>


### [12] [GRAND-HC: Graph-Refined Author Name Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01636)
*Yuanhao Sun,Zhouyang Jin,Yi Xu,Luoyi Fu,Jiaxin Ding,Xiaoying Gan,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了GRAND-HC，一个端到端的从零姓名消歧（SND）框架，通过谐波对比学习、图优化距离矩阵和轻量级论文压缩模块解决长尾作者分布和聚类数估计两大问题，已部署于十亿级学术数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SND方法存在两个关键局限：(1)固有的长尾作者分布导致表征学习偏差，引发对尾部作者的过度合并；(2)现有聚类数估计方法在长论文序列上不可靠，阻碍大规模部署。

Method: GRAND-HC框架包含四个核心组件：(1)通过合著者、合组织、同会议等关系构建异构论文图，使用图注意力网络作为嵌入骨干；(2)谐波对比学习（HCL）动态重加权训练损失以抑制对高产作者的过拟合，学习判别性嵌入；(3)图优化距离矩阵（GRDM）利用图拓扑优化成对距离，进一步防止尾部作者过度合并；(4)轻量级论文压缩模块（PCM）在不同规模下实现准确的聚类数估计。最后使用层次凝聚聚类输出最终簇。

Result: 大量实验表明GRAND-HC在宏F1指标上达到最先进水平，且已部署于十亿级学术数据库。

Conclusion: GRAND-HC通过结合图神经表征学习、对比学习重加权、图拓扑距离优化和模块化压缩策略，有效解决了SND中长期存在的长尾分布与聚类数估计问题，展现了大规模实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: From-Scratch Name Disambiguation (SND) groups papers sharing an ambiguous name into clusters of distinct real-world authors. Existing methods suffer from two critical limitations: (1) inherent long-tailed author distribution biases representation learning, causing over-merging of tail authors; (2) existing cluster number estimation methods are unreliable for long paper sequences, hindering large-scale deployment. We propose \textbf{GRAND-HC}, a complete end-to-end SND framework. We construct a heterogeneous paper graph via co-author, co-organization, and co-venue relations, using a graph attention network as the embedding backbone. \textbf{Harmony Contrastive Learning (HCL)} dynamically reweights training loss to suppress overfitting to prolific authors, learning discriminative embeddings. A \textbf{Graph-Refined Distance Matrix (GRDM)} leverages graph topology to optimize pairwise distances, further preventing tail author over-merging. Meanwhile, a lightweight \textbf{Paper Compression Module (PCM)} achieves accurate cluster number estimation across varying scales. Finally, Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering outputs the final clusters. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art macro F1 performance. GRAND-HC has been deployed in a billion-scale academic database. Source code: https://github.com/baokou-fw2/GRAND-HC.

</details>


### [13] [Imagine Before Retrieval: Prospective Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01642)
*Shuo Liu,Yutong Yang,Haohao Xiao,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SkillDreamer框架，通过模拟人类前瞻认知，先推断任务所需能力，再生成伪技能来弥合目标导向的任务查询与程序导向的技能之间的语义鸿沟，从而解决Query-Skill Misalignment问题，提升LLM代理的技能检索与任务执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能检索方法主要关注语义相关性匹配，但任务查询（目标导向）与技能描述（程序导向）天然存在视角差异，导致查询-技能错位（QSM）问题，使得代理难以将任务与合适技能关联起来。现有方法未充分探索该问题。

Method: SkillDreamer包含三个步骤：(1) 推断执行任务所需的能力； (2) 通过生成伪技能来想象如何实现这些能力； (3) 利用前瞻性信息弥合目标导向查询与执行导向技能之间的差距。

Result: 在SkillRet和SkillUsage基准上的实验验证了SkillDreamer在技能检索与端到端任务执行中的有效性，并展示了其在不同检索模型和检索管线上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillDreamer通过前瞻性推理有效缓解了查询-技能错位问题，为LLM代理的技能检索提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Skill retrieval has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for identifying the desirable execution guidelines from the skill gallery, thus equipping large language model (LLM) agents with the procedural knowledge to accomplish the specified task. To this end, most existing methods customize the retrieval model or reconfigure the retrieval pipeline to prioritize skills that are most semantically relevant to the task query. However, we empirically reveal that task queries and skills are naturally formulated from different perspectives, namely, objective-oriented and procedural-oriented, leading to an under-explored problem termed Query--Skill Misalignment (QSM). Clearly, it is daunting and even impossible to associate the desirable skills in the context of QSM, thus hindering the agent from correctly executing the task. As a remedy, inspired by human prospective cognition, we propose SkillDreamer, a novel framework to alleviate the negative impact of QSM problem. In brief, SkillDreamer first infers the capabilities necessary for task execution, then imagines how to realize these capabilities by generating pseudo skills, and finally leverages such prospective information to bridge the gap between objective-oriented task queries and execution-oriented skills. Extensive experiments on SkillRet and SkillUsage not only verify the effectiveness of SkillDreamer in both skill retrieval and end-to-end task execution, but also demonstrate its generalizability across diverse retrieval models and pipelines. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [14] [The Vocabulary Gap Is an Equity Gap: Register Mismatch in Retrieval Systems for Public-Benefits Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01645)
*Krish Sapru*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键问题：检索增强问答系统在公共福利资格场景中，因用户使用的日常语言与官方文件使用的机构语言之间存在"语域不匹配"，导致原本高性能的检索系统表现出严重的公平性缺陷。形式化语域下BM25检索Recall@5可达100%，但日常语域下骤降至44%；通过简单的可审计的plain-to-formal词表桥接，可将召回率恢复至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共福利资格问答系统旨在帮助民众导航福利资格信息，但检索系统所依据的官方文件使用机构注册语域，而用户通常以通俗、非正式甚至非母语英语提问。这种语域不匹配可能使高性能检索系统变成不公平的系统，而标准检索评估无法揭示这一问题。作者希望证明并量化这一失败模式，并提供可审计的缓解方案。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控基准，包含51条联邦福利资格规则和25个信息需求，每个需求同时以机构语域和日常用户语域表述，且黄金段落保持不变。对BM25、TF-IDF和术语图检索器进行对比评估。作者追踪失败机制到可测量的词汇差距（形式化查询与黄金段落的内容词重叠为0.63，日常查询仅为0.11，相差5.9倍），并提出一个刻意简单、可审计的plain-to-formal词表桥接方法进行缓解。

Result: 形式化语域评估近乎完美（Recall@5达96-100%），但日常语域检索大幅崩溃（Recall@5仅36-44%）。BM25的Recall@1从84%降至16%，Recall@5从100%降至44%，在相同信息需求上产生56个百分点的公平性差距。词表桥接方法将日常查询的BM25 Recall@5从44%提升到80%。

Conclusion: 本研究的贡献并非提出新的检索器，而是提供了一个评估协议、基准、机制诊断和透明缓解方案，用以应对标准检索评估掩盖的高风险社会影响失败模式。这一发现表明，在福利等高风险场景中，检索系统的评估必须考虑实际用户的语域，否则系统的表面高性能可能掩盖严重的社会不公平。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented question answering is increasingly used to help people navigate public-benefits eligibility, yet the documents these systems retrieve from are written in agency register while intended users often ask questions in plain, informal, or non-native English. We show that this register mismatch can turn a high-performing retrieval system into an inequitable one. We construct a controlled benchmark of 51 publicly documented federal benefit-eligibility rules and 25 information needs, each phrased in both agency register and plain user register while keeping the gold passage fixed. Across BM25, TF-IDF, and a term-graph retriever, formal-register evaluation is nearly perfect (Recall@5 96-100%), but plain-register retrieval collapses (Recall@5 36-44%). For BM25, Recall@1 falls from 84% to 16% and Recall@5 from 100% to 44%, a 56-point equity gap on identical information needs. We trace the mechanism to a measurable vocabulary gap: formal queries share 0.63 of their content terms with the gold passage, while plain queries share only 0.11, a 5.9x reduction. A deliberately simple, auditable plain-to-formal lexicon bridge recovers much of the failure, lifting plain-query BM25 Recall@5 from 44% to 80%. The contribution is not a new retriever; it is an evaluation protocol, benchmark, mechanistic diagnosis, and transparent mitigation for a high-stakes social-impact failure mode that standard retrieval evaluation hides.

</details>


### [15] [Not All Matches Are Equally Valuable: An Online Experiment of Retention-Focused Recommendation in a Job-Matching Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01652)
*Tatsuya Ute,Chiaki Ichimura,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究双面匹配平台（如求职平台）中推荐系统的留存优化问题。作者基于真实平台数据发现，近期匹配次数极少的用户流失风险显著更高，而已成功用户的额外匹配对留存边际价值有限。据此提出一个留存感知的推荐问题，并实现一种简单的后处理方法：对流失风险用户给予分数提升以增加其匹配机会。在线实验显示处理组用户流失率方向性低于对照组，但统计上不显著，且企业侧流失未恶化。这是首批在真实互惠求职平台上研究留存导向推荐的在线实验之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双边匹配平台的推荐系统通常仅优化即时互动信号（如点击率、回复率、匹配总数），但最大化匹配数可能与平台商业目标（如用户流失率、收入）不一致，尤其是当匹配少的用户流失风险显著更高时，需要研究留存导向的推荐策略。

Method: 首先通过真实求职平台的实证数据展示近期匹配数量与用户流失风险的关系；然后形式化留存感知的推荐问题；实现一种简单的后处理方法，在基线匹配导向的排序基础上，对流失风险用户给予分数加成以提升其匹配概率和留存率；最后在真实平台上开展在线实验评估效果。

Result: 在线实验中，处理组用户的流失率在方向上低于对照组，但估计效应未达到常规统计显著水平；同时公司侧流失未出现恶化迹象。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了留存导向推荐在真实互惠双面匹配平台上在线实验的首批证据，展示了通过后处理调整排名来缓解高流失风险用户退出的可行方法，并为未来在该方向上的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Recommender systems in two-sided matching platforms are commonly optimized for immediate engagement signals such as click-through rate, reply rate, or the total number of successful matches. However, in real-world marketplaces, maximizing matches alone may be misaligned with business goals such as user churn rate and platform revenue, especially when users with fewer matches are at substantially higher risk of churn. In this paper, we study a real job matching platform and show that users with very few recent matches are indeed much more likely to leave the platform, while additional matches for already successful users provide limited marginal value for retention. Motivated by this empirical finding, we formulate a retention-aware recommendation problem and implement a simple post-processing method that adjusts the baseline match-focused ranking to prevent user churn. Specifically, the implemented method gives a score boost to churn-risk users with the goal of increasing their likelihood of obtaining matches and improving retention. We evaluate this practical approach in an online experiment on a real job-matching platform. The treatment group showed directionally lower user churn than the control group, although the estimated effect was not statistically significant at conventional levels, while company-side churn showed no evidence of deterioration. To our knowledge, this is among the first online experimental studies to investigate retention-focused recommendation in a real reciprocal job-matching platform.

</details>


### [16] [MELON: A Large-Scale Dataset for Multi-Event Text-to-Long-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01654)
*Chan Hur,SeungWoo Song,Jeong-hun Hong,Won Jun Oh,Hyeyoung Park,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MELON，首个面向长视频多事件结构的文视频检索大规模数据集，并配套多事件感知损失函数，以填补现有短片段单事件数据集无法覆盖真实长视频检索场景的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文视频检索数据集主要基于短片段、单一主导事件，无法反映真实世界中长视频包含多个语义不同事件、单个文本查询可能对应多个非连续时间段的检索场景，存在明显的任务覆盖缺口。

Method: (1) 构建MELON数据集：为每个视频显式标注多个事件区间及其对应的文本描述，支持多事件理解在长未裁剪视频中的训练与评估；(2) 提出多事件感知损失（multi-event aware loss），引导模型区分完整事件匹配与部分事件匹配，提升检索精度。

Result: 提出的多事件感知损失在检索准确率上带来显著提升；MELON数据集与损失函数共同为长视频复杂场景下的文视频检索建立了坚实基础。

Conclusion: MELON及其配套损失将文视频检索从短片段单事件范式扩展到长视频多事件场景，为未来研究提供了更贴近实际应用的评测环境。

Abstract: Existing text-video retrieval datasets primarily consist of short-form clips containing a single dominant event. While suitable for measuring basic vision-language alignment, they are limited in capturing real-world retrieval scenarios, where long-form videos naturally contain multiple semantically distinct events and a single text query may correspond to several non-contiguous temporal segments. To bridge this gap, we introduce MELON, the first large-scale dataset designed to extend text-video retrieval to long-form videos featuring complex, multi-event structures. MELON explicitly annotates multiple event intervals per video along with their corresponding textual descriptions, enabling both training and evaluation of multi-event understanding in long, untrimmed videos. In addition, we propose a multi-event aware loss that encourages models to differentiate between full-event and partial-event matches, yielding substantial improvements in retrieval accuracy. Together, the MELON dataset and our proposed loss establish a robust foundation for expanding text-to-video retrieval to complex long-form scenarios and provide a more realistic evaluation setting for future research in the field.

</details>


### [17] [From Feature Interaction to Feature Transport - A Unified Block for Scalable Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01655)
*Zichen Luo,Jiachen Guo,Keming Gu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT（上下文残差自适应特征传输模块），将深度统一推荐视为离散的上下文条件表示演化过程，使非序列上下文特征成为表示演化的主动控制器。在TAAC2026广告推荐竞赛中达到测试AUC 0.838090，超过此前榜单最佳0.83798，并通过堆叠六个block（0.838148）和增加隐藏维度（0.838106）验证了深度与宽度扩展的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一推荐模型的交互中心设计主要关注每层内混合异构token，但可扩展的统一推荐还需控制意图信息如何在堆叠块间携带、过滤和保留，这一点尚未被充分解决。

Method: 受流式表示动力学启发，提出特征传输视角，将深度统一推荐视为离散的上下文条件表示演化过程。CRAFT模块将非序列特征汇总为可靠性感知的上下文场，并利用该上下文场为意图表示和序列表示生成残差位移与记忆保持信号，实现非序列上下文对表示演化的主动控制。

Result: TAAC2026竞赛中测试AUC为0.838090，超过此前最优0.83798；堆叠至六层达到0.838148，增大隐藏维度达0.838106。实验表明CRAFT在深度与宽度两个维度上均有扩展收益。

Conclusion: CRAFT验证了特征传输范式的有效性、可扩展性及泛化潜力，为统一推荐模型的架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unified recommendation models aim to jointly model non-sequential multi-field features and sequential user behaviors, but existing interaction-centric designs mainly focus on mixing heterogeneous tokens within each layer. We argue that scalable unified recommendation also requires controlling how intent information is carried, filtered, and preserved across stacked blocks. Inspired by flow-based representation dynamics, we introduce feature transport, a view that treats deep unified recommendation as a discrete context-conditioned representation evolution process. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual Residual Adaptive Feature Transport block, which summarizes non-sequential features into a reliability-aware contextual field and uses it to generate residual displacement and memory-preserving signals for intent and sequence representations. In this way, non-sequential context acts as an active controller of representation evolution rather than a passive object of interaction. In the TAAC2026 advertising recommendation competition, CRAFT achieves a test AUC of 0.838090, surpassing the previous leaderboard-best score of 0.83798. Scaling experiments further show that CRAFT benefits from both depth and width expansion: stacking CRAFT to six blocks improves test AUC to 0.838148, while increasing the hidden dimension reaches 0.838106. These results demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and generalization potential of the feature transport paradigm. Source code: https://github.com/AshleyLuo001/CRAFT

</details>


### [18] [NeoMME: A Single-Tower Multimodal-Native Multilingual Foundation Encoder for Efficient Fine-Tuning and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01657)
*Aurélien Lac,Tony Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NeoMME是一个260M和800M参数的、从零预训练的多模态多语言双向编码器家族，将多语言文本与原始图像补丁统一在同一Transformer编码器中处理，并在ViDoRe v3基准上以更小的参数量和更高的吞吐量超越了更大规模的视觉检索模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索器（如ColPali）将生成式视觉语言模型（VLM）重新用作编码器，继承了VLM的大量参数与计算开销，而这些开销对于非生成式任务（如检索）而言是多余的。作者旨在设计一个专门的、从零训练的双向编码器，以避免这种低效。

Method: NeoMME采用单一双向Transformer编码器架构，统一处理多语言文本与原始图像补丁。模型使用掩码离散扩散文本目标从零预训练，多模态样本以可见图像补丁为条件。模型支持16,384 token上下文，可编码两张4K UHD图像。下游通过联合训练密集与晚交互（late-interaction）检索头进行微调。还引入了分层token池化与非对称量化技术压缩嵌入。

Result: NeoMME-Retriever 260M在ViDoRe v3上达到0.523 nDCG@10，超越所有低于800M参数的评估模型；800M版本达到0.556。在同为2048x2048图像输入下，NeoMME-260M在NVIDIA L40S上的编码吞吐量约为ColModernVBERT的2倍。分层token池化与非对称量化可将晚交互多模态文档嵌入压缩255倍，同时保持超过95%的基线nDCG@10。

Conclusion: NeoMME证明从零预训练的小型双向编码器架构能够在视觉文档检索任务上以更高效率匹敌甚至超越更大规模的VLM衍生模型，并在模型压缩方面实现显著收益。模型已在Apache 2.0许可下通过Hugging Face Transformers开源发布。

Abstract: Multimodal models often build on architectures designed for generative vision-language modeling, typically combining separately pretrained vision encoders with causal language models. Visual document retrievers such as ColPali repurpose these models as encoders, carrying over the parameter and compute overhead of a VLM for a non-generative task.
  We introduce NeoMME, a family of 260M and 800M-parameter Multimodal and Multilingual bidirectional Encoders that process multilingual text and raw image patches in a single bidirectional Transformer encoder. Both models are pretrained from scratch with a masked discrete-diffusion text objective, conditioned on visible image patches for multimodal examples. Both support a 16,384-token context, enough to encode up to two standard 4K UHD images.
  To demonstrate its downstream capabilities, we fine-tune NeoMME with jointly trained dense and late-interaction heads. On the ViDoRe v3 benchmark, the resulting NeoMME-Retriever 260M outperforms all evaluated models strictly below 800M parameters with 0.523 nDCG@10, while NeoMME-Retriever 800M reaches 0.556. At a matched 2048x2048 image input size on an NVIDIA L40S, NeoMME-260M encodes pages with about 2x the throughput of ColModernVBERT. Hierarchical token pooling and asymmetric quantization compress late-interaction multimodal document embeddings by 255x while preserving over 95% of baseline nDCG@10. We contribute NeoMME to Hugging Face Transformers and release the pretrained backbone and retrieval-compatible checkpoints under Apache 2.0 at https://hf.co/collections/Hcompany/neomme.

</details>


### [19] [KGVoyager: Knowledge Graph Agnostic Question Answering via Agentic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01780)
*Essam Wisam,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KGVoyager是一种图谱无关的智能体架构，通过think-act-observe循环动态发现图结构和语义，仅需查询端点即可将自然语言问题转换为SPARQL查询，无需预定义本体或训练样本。在四个基准上F1提升约8点，成本和运行时间各降低约22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域特定的知识图谱问答（KGQA）在RDF图上仍具挑战性，因为正式本体和人工标注的text-SPARQL对常常不可用。现有方法依赖预定义本体或训练示例，限制了其在真实世界端点上的适用性。

Method: 提出KGVoyager，一种KG-agnostic的智能体架构，采用think-act-observe循环，集成搜索、探索和执行工具。动态将词项映射到图IRI，揭示图结构，并通过执行反馈来细化查询。仅需轻量级类索引（class index）即可工作，而不需要预存在的本体或示例。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，KGVoyager相比先前最先进方法在F1分数上提升约8个百分点，同时将成本和运行时间各降低约22%。

Conclusion: KGVoyager验证了仅依赖查询端点和轻量级类索引的智能体方法能够在无需正式本体和训练样本的条件下实现更优的KGQA性能，同时降低资源消耗，使KGQA对更多真实世界的知识图谱端点更加实用。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) over RDF graphs remains challenging in domain-specific settings, where formal ontologies and curated text-SPARQL pairs are often unavailable. We present KGVoyager, a KG-agnostic agentic architecture that generates SPARQL queries from natural language questions by dynamically discovering graph structure and semantics, requiring only a query endpoint of the underlying graph. Using a think-act-observe loop with search, exploration, and execution tools, KGVoyager maps terms to graph IRIs, uncovers structure, and refines queries through execution feedback - all without pre-existing ontologies or examples. Unlike the prior state of the art, KGVoyager requires only a lightweight class index which renders it applicable for far more real-world endpoints. Across four benchmarks, KGVoyager improves F1 by ~8 points while cutting cost and runtime by ~22% each.

</details>


### [20] [Index-Free Dynamic Edge Retrieval with Energy-Tail-Aware Partial Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01820)
*Mohammad Arif Rasyidi,Omar Alhussein*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ETAR是一种面向动态最大内积搜索(MIPS)的无索引方法，通过将查询坐标中平方值最大的分量用于估计相似度、跳过低幅值尾部并使用低精度紧凑表示与全精度重排序，在保持简单更新的同时显著降低查询成本。在九组静态数据集上平均达到99.2%的Recall@10，比精确扫描快4倍以上，在移动设备上快6.9倍，并在五组流式工作负载下始终保持100%的Recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态MIPS需要在数据插入、替换和删除时仍能返回查询点积最大的K个向量。全向量扫描更新简单但查询成本高，而索引方法虽降低查询成本但在更新时需要维护额外结构。作者希望设计一种方法，在不引入索引维护开销的前提下减少查询工作量，从而兼顾查询效率与简单更新。

Method: ETAR为无索引方法，其核心策略包括：(1) 选取查询中平方值最大的坐标，使其覆盖总平方范数的大部分，并将剩余坐标视作低幅值尾部并跳过；(2) 用这些保留坐标以紧凑的低精度表示估计相似度；(3) 对跳过坐标的影响进行校正；(4) 用全精度向量对固定数量的候选进行重排序。

Result: 在五轮运行、九组静态数据集上平均达到99.2%的Recall@10，在代表性设置下比精确扫描快4倍以上；在ARM移动设备上对四种合成分布可达6.9倍加速；在五组流式工作负载的每个测量点都保持100%的Recall@10，无需索引重建。

Conclusion: ETAR为动态MIPS提供了一种实用的折中方案：在不引入索引结构的条件下显著降低查询成本，同时保持简单的无索引更新，在召回率和速度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Dynamic maximum inner-product search (MIPS) returns the $K$ stored vectors with the largest dot products with a query while allowing the dataset to change through insertions, replacements, and deletions. For edge retrieval, the challenge is to achieve high recall and fast queries without making updates expensive. Full-vector scanning keeps updates simple but compares each query with every stored vector, while indexed methods reduce query cost at the expense of maintaining additional structures during updates. We propose ETAR, an index-free method that reduces query work while preserving simple updates. ETAR keeps the query coordinates with the largest squared values until they cover most of its total squared magnitude and treats the rest as a low-magnitude tail. It estimates similarity from the retained coordinates using a compact lower-precision representation, corrects for skipped coordinates, and reranks a fixed number of candidates using full-precision vectors. Across five runs on nine static datasets, ETAR averages 99.2% Recall@10, the fraction of exact top-10 results recovered, while running over 4$\times$ faster than exact scanning at a representative setting. This speedup also extends to an ARM-based mobile device, where ETAR is up to 6.9$\times$ faster across four synthetic distributions. Under five streaming workloads, it maintains 100% Recall@10 at every measured point without index rebuilds. Overall, ETAR offers a practical middle ground for dynamic MIPS by reducing query cost while retaining simple, index-free updates. Code is available at https://github.com/arasyi/etar-mips.

</details>


### [21] [MERGED: Multimodal Entity Resolution via Generated Expert Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01913)
*You-Lin Chen,Kyoungjun Park,Bin Xu,Prithviraj Sen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MERGED是一个从大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）教师蒸馏到紧凑7B参数学生模型的蒸馏框架，无需人工标注即可将标签和结构化推理同时转移。通过多教师标注协议对和元评判处理分歧，结合监督微调和直接偏好优化训练。在多语言电商数据集上，学生模型在PR-AUC上比基于人工标签训练的模型提升13.79%，以6倍更低的成本超越更大规模的Qwen2.5-32B-VL基线6.32%，标签-推理对齐度提升超10%。MERGED支持从已有检查点快速适应新关系定义，仅需1万样本即可数天内完成新定义的适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品实体解析中关系定义随业务需求不断演化，但传统适应方式依赖缓慢、昂贵且常有噪声、缺乏推理过程的人工标注。大型视觉语言模型零样本可以立即适应新定义并提供推理，但其成本和延迟在生产规模下不可接受。因此需要一种在不依赖人工标注的前提下，从大模型蒸馏出紧凑学生模型，同时转移标签和结构化推理的方法。

Method: MERGED采用多教师蒸馏架构：多个大型VLM教师对每个产品对进行标注并阐述推理过程。教师之间一致的对子用于监督微调（SFT）；教师之间存在分歧时，由一个元评判（meta-judge）模型将分歧转化为偏好对，用于直接偏好优化（DPO）训练。学生模型为7B参数的小型VLM，无需任何人工标注。

Result: 在多语言电商数据集上，以人工标注真值评估：学生模型较同一骨干基于人工标签训练提升PR-AUC 13.79%；超越更大的Qwen2.5-32B-VL基线6.32%，同时成本降低6倍；标签与推理对齐度比Qwen2.5-32B-VL高出超10%。从既有检查点重新应用MERGED适应新关系定义仅需1万样本，PR-AUC较零样本提升6.97%，且优于从零训练。

Conclusion: MERGED实现了对演化中关系定义的快速适应，从传统数月缩短至数天，且成本与延迟满足大规模工业部署需求，证实了无人工标注的大模型到紧凑模型的知识与推理蒸馏路径的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: In product entity resolution, relationship definitions constantly evolve with business needs, yet adapting to each change traditionally requires slow, costly human annotation that is often noisy and carries no reasoning. Large vision-language models (VLMs) prompted zero-shot can adapt to a new definition immediately and supply the reasoning that human labels lack, but their cost and latency are prohibitive at production scale. We present MERGED, a distillation framework that transfers not just labels but structured reasoning from large teacher VLMs into a compact 7B-parameter student, requiring no human annotation. Multiple teachers label each product pair and articulate the reasoning behind their decision: agreement pairs supply supervised fine-tuning, while disagreements are resolved by a meta-judge into preference pairs for Direct Preference Optimization. Evaluated against human-labeled ground truth on a multilingual e-commerce dataset, the resulting student improves PR-AUC by 13.79% over the same backbone trained on human labels and surpasses the larger Qwen2.5-32B-VL baseline by 6.32% at 6x lower cost, while also yielding tighter label-reasoning alignment (over 10% above Qwen2.5-32B-VL). Moreover, re-applying MERGED from an existing checkpoint adapts to a new relationship definition with only 10K samples, improving PR-AUC by 6.97% over zero-shot and outperforming from-scratch training. MERGED enables rapid adaptation to evolving relationship definitions, supporting a new one in days rather than months, at a cost and latency suitable for large-scale industrial deployment.

</details>


### [22] [SPAR: Enhancing Industrial-Scale Generative POI Recommendation via Real-World Spatial Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02062)
*Fangye Wang,Yunjin Gu,Haowen Lin,Yifang Yuan,Song Yang,Xiaojiang Zhou,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPAR框架，在生成式POI推荐中显式注入真实城市空间知识（经纬度、距离、可达性），通过三层协同机制（空间内在SID编码、多粒度地理连续预训练、任务向量锚定的微调）将地理感知与行为协作信号统一于语义ID生成空间中，在多个公开与工业数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式POI推荐方法在行为序列和协作信号定义的兴趣空间中操作，地理信息仅作为SID的文本属性被动参与，缺乏显式机制学习或保持城市地点之间由距离、方向和可达性定义的空间关系，导致预测虽行为上合理却偏离用户实时位置。作者认为此类服务需将真实城市空间知识注入兴趣空间，而非仅从行为中推断地理。

Method: 提出SPAR统一框架，包含三个协同阶段：(1) 令牌化层面的Spatially-Intrinsic SID（SI-SID），将经纬度编码为正弦地理嵌入并与文本语义嵌入融合，再经RQ-Kmeans生成语义与地理双一致的标识符；(2) 认知层面的Multi-Granular Geospatial CPT（MG-CPT），在25个地理数据集（分基础属性、成对关系、城市尺度导航三层）上连续预训练基础LLM，使散乱POI凝聚为连通的城市空间；(3) 适配层面的Task-Vector Anchored SFT（TV-SFT），将习得空间知识锚定为冻结的参数空间任务向量，防止行为微调时灾难性遗忘，从而融合两类空间。

Result: 在两个公开数据集和四个工业规模数据集上的定量与可视化实验表明SPAR有效，验证了显式注入城市空间知识相比仅从行为推断地理的优越性。

Conclusion: SPAR证实将真实城市空间知识显式编码进语义ID、持续预训练与任务向量锚定微调的三阶段协同方案，能有效弥合行为兴趣空间与地理空间之间的鸿沟，为生成式POI推荐提供了空间感知的新范式。

Abstract: Generative Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation, autoregressively generating a target POI's semantic ID (SID), holds great promise for Location-Based Services, where a recommendation helps only if the user can reach it. Yet, existing methods operate within an interest space defined by behavior sequences and collaborative signals, where geography enters only as a textual attribute of the SID, leaving no explicit mechanism to learn or preserve how urban places are related by distance, direction, and reachability; their predictions are thus behaviorally plausible yet far from the user's real-time location. We argue that such services require injecting real urban spatial knowledge into the interest space, rather than inferring geography from behavior alone. Hence, we propose SPAR, a unified framework whose three synergistic stages jointly construct, cultivate, and preserve urban spatial knowledge: (1) at the tokenization level, Spatially-Intrinsic SID (SI-SID) explicitly encodes longitude--latitude coordinates into a sinusoidal geospatial embedding and fuses it with the textual semantic embedding, producing identifiers via RQ-Kmeans that are simultaneously semantically and geographically consistent; (2) at the cognition level, Multi-Granular Geospatial CPT (MG-CPT) continually pre-trains the base LLM on 25 curated geospatial datasets organized into three tiers of basic attributes, pairwise relations, and city-scale navigation, so that scattered POIs cohere into a connected urban space; and (3) at the adaptation level, Task-Vector Anchored SFT (TV-SFT) anchors the acquired spatial knowledge as a frozen parameter-space task vector to prevent its catastrophic forgetting during behavioral fine-tuning, thereby fusing the two spaces. Extensive quantitative and visualization experiments on two public and four industrial-scale datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SPAR.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Modality Harmony: Orthogonal Purification and Topology-Guided MoE for Conflict-Aware Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02152)
*Jialin Liu,Zhaorui Zhang,Ray C. C. Cheung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OrthoRec提出了一种面向冲突感知的多模态推荐系统，通过协作引导正交纯化（CGOP）将多模态特征几何解耦为平行与正交于协作锚点的方向，并自适应截断正交噪声；同时设计拓扑感知路由MoE（TAR-MoE）以解耦sigmoid门控突破softmax零和瓶颈，自主决定各纯化模态的注入规模，并引入safe-SSL目标动态惩罚矛盾对的强制对比对齐。在三个真实Amazon数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统普遍基于有缺陷的"模态和谐"假设，认为多模态特征天然有益且与用户协同交互模式严格对齐。然而现实中模态-拓扑冲突普遍存在（如欺骗性视觉标题和语义不匹配），盲目整合噪声模态会污染纯协作空间，导致严重表示失真。

Method: 提出OrthoRec框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 协作引导正交纯化（CGOP）：将多模态特征几何解耦为平行于和正交于纯协作锚点的方向，通过能量保持归一化自适应截断正交噪声，修正欺骗性语义方向同时保留模态固有表征能力；(2) 拓扑感知路由MoE（TAR-MoE）：以协作拓扑为引导，采用解耦sigmoid门控打破传统softmax注意力的零和瓶颈，自主确定每个纯化模态的注入规模；(3) safe-SSL目标：动态惩罚矛盾对的强制对比对齐。

Result: 在三个真实Amazon数据集上的实验表明，OrthoRec在常规性能上持续优于近期基线的同时，在模态噪声和物品稀疏性条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OrthoRec通过显式建模并处理模态与拓扑之间的冲突，而非盲目融合多模态特征，有效减轻了噪声模态对协作空间的污染，验证了冲突感知设计在多模态推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Recommender Systems (MRSs) typically rely on a flawed "modality harmony" assumption, presuming that multimodal features are inherently beneficial and strictly aligned with users' collaborative interaction patterns. However, modality-topology conflicts are ubiquitous in real-world scenarios due to deceptive visual clickbaits and mismatched semantics. Blindly integrating these noisy modalities inevitably pollutes the pristine collaborative space, causing severe representation distortion. To address this, we propose Orthogonal purification and topology-guided MoE for conflict-aware multimodal Recommendation (OrthoRec). At its core, OrthoRec introduces Collaborative-Guided Orthogonal Purification (CGOP), which geometrically decouples multimodal features into directions parallel and orthogonal to a pure collaborative anchor. By adaptively truncating the orthogonal noise with an energy-preserving normalization, CGOP rectifies deceptive semantic directions while preserving the modality's intrinsic representation capacity. Furthermore, we design a Topology-Aware Routing Mixture-of-Experts (TAR-MoE). Guided by the collaborative topology, TAR-MoE employs decoupled sigmoid gating to break the zero-sum bottleneck of traditional softmax attention, autonomously determining the injection scale for each purified modality. Finally, a safe-SSL objective is introduced to dynamically penalize the forced contrastive alignment of contradictory pairs. Experiments on three real-world Amazon datasets show that OrthoRec consistently outperforms competitive recent baselines and exhibits improved robustness under modality noise and item sparsity.

</details>


### [24] [GenCAR: Generative Counterfactual Alignment with Risk-Controlled Selection for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02162)
*Qianqian Wang,Yunshan Li,Jiawen Zeng,Wenwu Gong,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenCAR，将OOD推荐中的服务问题形式化为α-有效反事实推荐（α-VCR）问题，在反事实监督下保留候选支持集的同时控制代理标签假发现率（FDR），并通过偏好锚定、信任半径过滤和保形p值的Benjamini-Hochberg选择实现校准的集合选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD推荐方法主要改进排序或构造反事实候选，但未控制服务集合中代理标签的FDR，导致在分布偏移下难以平衡效用与风险。因此需要一个能够在反事实监督下同时保证候选质量和统计风险控制的推荐框架。

Method: GenCAR包含三个关键组件：(1) 固定稳定偏好表示同时对环境因素进行干预，实现偏好基础的反事实监督；(2) 通过偏好锚点和信任半径过滤，约束离线大语言模型生成的候选提议；(3) 使用保形p值进行Benjamini-Hochberg选择，控制代理标签FDR。

Result: 理论上，论文给出了条件反事实逼近误差的界，并证明了在可交换性和正回归依赖条件下代理标签FDR的有限样本、分布无关控制，在任意依赖条件下具有Benjamini-Yekutieli保证。实验审计了实际代理假发现比例，表明GenCAR在多个基准上持续增强OOD候选恢复能力。

Conclusion: GenCAR通过将反事实监督与保形校准的选择程序相结合，有效解决了OOD推荐中控制代理标签FDR的挑战，在提升OOD候选恢复性能的同时提供了严格的统计保证，兼顾了效用与风险之间的平衡。

Abstract: Serving useful recommendations under distribution shift is crucial for balancing utility and risk in out-of-distribution (OOD) recommendation. However, most existing OOD methods improve ranking or construct counterfactual candidates without controlling the proxy-label false discovery rate (FDR) of the served set. In this work, we formulate OOD serving as the $α$-Valid Counterfactual Recommendation ($α$-VCR) problem to retain candidate support learned from counterfactual supervision while controlling proxy-label FDR, and propose GenCAR, which couples preference-grounded counterfactual supervision with calibrated set selection. In particular, GenCAR fixes the stable-preference representation while intervening on the environmental factor, grounds offline large language model proposals through preference anchors and trust-radius filtering, and uses conformal $p$-values for Benjamini--Hochberg selection. We theoretically bound conditional counterfactual approximation error and prove finite-sample, distribution-free control of proxy-label FDR under exchangeability and positive regression dependence, with a Benjamini--Yekutieli guarantee under arbitrary dependence. Extensive experiments audit realized proxy false discovery proportions and demonstrate that GenCAR consistently enhances OOD candidate recovery across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [25] [Group-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Evidence Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02188)
*June Park,Jun Kwon,Jonghyo Kim,Jongwuk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GAREN是一种面向推理密集型检索的群感知自适应检索方法，通过将文档组织成语义连贯的组并以组级为单位探索语料库图，解决了已有方法在文档级迭代扩展中面临的召回率受限问题，在BRIGHT基准上相比最强基线提升最高达8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理密集型检索中，相关文档很少出现在初始候选集中，系统面临有界召回（bounded recall）问题。现有方法在语料库图上以文档级别迭代扩展候选池，孤立地检查每个邻居，容易漂移到语料库的狭窄区域，导致检索效果受限。

Method: GAREN通过组级（group-level）扩展来探索语料库图。具体而言：(1) 基于语料库图中的文档连接关系，将文档组织成语义上连贯且可区分的组；(2) 每组的信息指示通过扩展该组可访问的内容，提供超越单个文档级别信号的引导；(3) 每次迭代中，组级导航器识别有前景的扩展方向；(4) 从被选中的组中检索文档；(5) 应用文档级重排序器评估更新后的候选集。

Result: 在BRIGHT基准上的大量实验表明，GAREN相比最强基线实现了最高8.0%的改进。

Conclusion: 组级扩展策略能有效解决文档级迭代扩展导致的窄域漂移问题，为推理密集型检索提供了更稳健的候选集覆盖方案。源码已公开。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval addresses queries whose relevance cannot be identified by surface-level matching, thereby requiring multi-step reasoning. Because relevant documents rarely appear in the initial candidate set, retrieval systems suffer from the bounded recall problem. Existing methods iteratively expand a candidate pool at the document level over a corpus graph, examining each neighbor in isolation and drifting toward a narrow region of the corpus. To address this problem, we propose Group-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Evidence Navigation (GAREN), which explores the corpus graph through group-level expansion. GAREN organizes documents into semantically coherent and distinguishable groups based on their connections in the corpus graph. The information in each group indicates what can be accessed by expanding through it, providing guidance beyond individual document-level signals. At each iteration, GAREN uses a group-level navigator to identify promising expansion directions, retrieves documents from the selected groups, and applies a document-level reranker to evaluate the updated candidate set. Extensive experiments show that GAREN achieves up to 8.0% improvement over the strongest baseline on BRIGHT. The source code is available at https://github.com/KJ12124/GAREN

</details>


### [26] [Genuine Information Needs of Social Scientists Looking for Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02303)
*Andrea Papenmeier,Thomas Krämer,Tanja Friedrich,Daniel Hienert,Dagmar Kern*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过对72名社会科学研究人员的在线调查，分析其在寻找研究数据时的真实信息需求，并将其归为"主题、元数据和意图"三类，与现有的元数据模型和搜索系统功能进行对比，发现用户数据需求与元数据层面及搜索系统提供的可能性之间存在不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学研究数据的发布被广泛预期能促进其重用和启发新研究，但数据搜索系统中用户的复杂信息需求与系统搜索能力之间存在冲突，而搜索能力又在很大程度上取决于描述数据的元数据方案。因此需要深入了解用户真实的信息需求，以改进数据搜索系统的设计。

Method: 采用在线问卷调查方法，对72名社会科学研究人员进行调查，让参与者以"向同事寻求帮助"的方式表达其对研究数据的个性化信息需求。随后对这些信息需求进行分析，将不同组成部分归入主题（topic）、元数据（metadata）和意图（intention）三个类别，并将这些类别与现有研究数据元数据模型以及现有数据搜索系统提供的搜索和过滤功能进行比较。

Result: 研究发现用户在表达数据需求时涉及主题、元数据和意图等多个维度，但现有元数据模型及数据搜索系统所提供的搜索、过滤功能与用户的真实需求之间存在明显的不匹配。

Conclusion: 现有数据搜索系统在元数据层面和搜索功能上未能充分满足社会科学研究人员对研究数据的复杂信息需求，需要改进元数据方案和搜索系统设计，以更好地支持用户的数据检索与重用。

Abstract: Publishing research data is widely expected to increase its reuse and to inspire new research. In the social sciences, data from surveys, interviews, polls, and statistics are primary resources for research. There is a long tradition to collect and offer research data in data archives and online repositories. Researchers use these systems to identify data relevant to their research. However, especially in data search, users' complex information needs seem to collide with the capabilities of data search systems. The search capabilities, in turn, depend to a high degree upon the metadata schemes used to describe the data. In this research, we conducted an online survey with 72 social science researchers who expressed their individual information needs for research data like they would do when asking a colleague for help. We analyzed these information needs and attributed their different components to the categories: topic, metadata, and intention. We compared these categories and their content to existing metadata models of research data and the search and filter opportunities offered in existing data search systems. We found a mismatch between what users have as a requirement for their data and what is offered on metadata level and search system possibilities.

</details>


### [27] [Counter-GEO-Bench: Evaluating Defenses Against Information-Distorting Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02316)
*Bing Zheng,Zongyao Zhao,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Counter-GEO-Bench，一个用于评估生成式搜索地理信息滥用（GEO）对抗防御手段的基准测试，包含247个经过人工验证的查询及其信息保留/扭曲的GEO改写。测试显示三种现成防御方法最多仅能将攻击成功率降低5.7%，而提出的轻量级基线C-GEO Guard可将攻击成功率相对降低47.6%且几乎没有效用损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEO技术可使内容生产者提高在生成式搜索引擎中的可见度，但攻击者可利用相同技术发布表面正常的GEO优化文档，被受害LLM检索并合成为扭曲答案，从而定向传播错误信息。然而现有文献中缺乏在受控条件下评估针对此类威胁的防御机制的基准测试。

Method: 构建Counter-GEO-Bench基准：包含247个人工验证且质量门槛筛选的查询，配对应信息保留与信息扭曲的GEO改写；在三个受害LLM上评估防御方法在攻击成功率（ASR）、误报率与回答质量方面的表现。同时对三种现成防御方法与一个提出的轻量级基线C-GEO Guard进行对比评估。

Result: 在Counter-GEO-Bench上，三种现成防御（Granite Guardian、Llama Guard 3和NeMo Self-Check Fact-Checking）最多仅相对降低5.7%的攻击成功率，其中Granite Guardian的降低在统计上不显著。而提出的轻量级防御基线C-GEO Guard可将ASR相对降低47.6%，且近乎零效用损失。

Conclusion: 基于安全分类法的传统护栏主要针对策略违规内容，而GEO错误信息以流畅的信息性内容形态绕过它们。C-GEO Guard的成功表明该威胁是可攻克的，但需要专门面向GEO对抗性信息设计的防御机制。该基准为后续防御研究提供了受控的评估平台。

Abstract: Generative engine optimization (GEO) enables content producers to increase the visibility of their web pages in generative search engines, but the same techniques can deliver targeted misinformation when adversaries publish ordinary-looking GEO-optimized documents that victim large language models (LLMs) retrieve and synthesize into distorted answers. No existing benchmark evaluates defenses against this threat under controlled conditions. Therefore, we present Counter-GEO-Bench, a defense benchmark that pairs 247 human-verified, quality-gated queries with information-preserving and information-distorting GEO rewrites, and evaluates defenses on attack success rate (ASR), false positive rate, and answer quality across three victim LLMs. Under Counter-GEO-Bench, three off-the-shelf defenses (Granite Guardian, Llama Guard 3, and NeMo Self-Check Fact-Checking) reduce ASR by at most 5.7% relative, while Granite Guardian's reduction is not statistically significant. Safety-taxonomy guardrails target policy violations, while GEO misinformation passes through them as fluent informational content. To this end, a lightweight benchmark baseline, C-GEO Guard, is proposed, reducing ASR by 47.6% relative with near-zero utility loss, which proves threat tractable.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Test-Time Inference for Text2Cypher with Trace Budgeting and Selective Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02324)
*Makbule Gulcin Ozsoy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对Text2Cypher任务提出自适应测试时推理策略，包括自适应trace预算分配和选择性执行引导精炼，在不降低生成质量的前提下显著减少推理开销。实验表明该方法可将平均生成预算降低30.7%，推理时间降低21-25%，且精炼能力可跨模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化数据库的自然语言界面中生成的Cypher查询仍存在语法错误、违反数据库模式或执行失败等问题。现有测试时推理策略通常使用固定推理预算和统一精炼策略，导致简单问题也被分配不必要的计算资源，缺乏针对问题复杂度的自适应能力。

Method: 提出两种自适应策略：(1) 自适应trace预算分配，根据问题难度动态调整候选生成预算；(2) 选择性执行引导精炼，仅在预测进一步推理有益时才应用修正。实验在Gemma-2-9B和Qwen-2.5-7B上进行验证。

Result: 自适应trace预算分配将平均生成预算降低30.7%，墙钟推理时间降低21-25%，同时保持可比生成质量。选择性精炼在保留完整精炼几乎全部执行成功增益的同时，仅使执行成功率下降0.2-0.5%。此外，单一修正模型（Gemma-4）能有效精炼不同家族模型的输出，证明精炼能力跨家族迁移。

Conclusion: 自适应测试时推理策略能够根据问题难度动态分配推理资源，在效率和生成质量之间实现更优权衡，且修正模型的跨家族泛化能力为进一步降低部署成本提供了可能性。

Abstract: Large language models have enabled natural language interfaces for structured databases, but generated queries may still contain syntactic errors, violate database schemas, or fail during execution. Test-time inference strategies improve generation reliability without additional training, but existing approaches often use fixed inference budgets and uniform refinement strategies, leading to unnecessary computation across questions with different complexity levels. In this work, we investigate adaptive test-time inference for Text2Cypher and introduce two strategies: adaptive trace budgeting, which dynamically adjusts the candidate generation budget based on question difficulty, and selective execution-guided refinement, which applies correction only when additional inference is expected to be beneficial. Experiments on Gemma-2-9B and Qwen-2.5-7B show that adaptive trace budgeting reduces the average generation budget by 30.7% and wall-clock inference time by 21-25% while maintaining comparable generation quality. Selective refinement preserves nearly all execution success gains of full refinement, reducing execution success by only 0.2-0.5% while avoiding unnecessary refinement for simpler questions. Experiments show that a single correction model (Gemma-4) effectively refines outputs from a different model family, suggesting refinement transfers across families.

</details>


### [29] [ViSAR: Training-Free Adaptive-$k$ Retrieval for Visual Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02486)
*Adrien Mialland,Marc Plantevit,Julien Gallois,Céline Robardet*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ViSAR是一种无需训练的适应性k值检索方法，用于文档视觉问答中的延迟交互式视觉文档检索。它在嵌入空间中构建查询条件化的页面级相似度矩阵，动态确定要检索的页面数量，将RAG延迟降低高达58.7%，同时保持或提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DocVQA中的RAG方法通常检索固定数量的页面，忽略了查询复杂度的差异。固定top-k检索会增加LVLM的推理延迟，并可能因引入无关页面而降低答案准确率。因此需要一种能够根据查询复杂度动态调整检索页面数量的方法。

Method: ViSAR直接在嵌入空间中操作，构建一个查询条件化的页面级相似度矩阵，该矩阵突出查询相关的语义信息，并动态决定需要检索的页面数量。该方法无需训练，可适配多种编码器和LVLM。

Result: 在多种编码器和LVLM组合下，ViSAR能够检索紧凑且查询适配的页面集合，相比固定top-k和自适应检索启发式方法，可将RAG延迟降低最多58.7%，同时保持或提升答案准确率。此外，研究还发现相似度矩阵结构与答案准确率存在相关性。

Conclusion: ViSAR是一种高效的训练-free自适应检索方法，兼顾了检索效率与答案质量，同时相似度矩阵与准确率的相关性为未来检索质量感知的文档理解研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) often leverages Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), where late-interaction encoders are commonly used to identify document pages relevant to a user query, before answer generation by a Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM). Existing approaches typically retrieve a fixed top-$k$ number of pages regardless of query complexity, which increases LVLM latency and may degrade answer accuracy. We introduce ViSAR (Visual Semantic Activation Retrieval), a training-free adaptive-$k$ retrieval method for late-interaction visual document retrieval. ViSAR operates directly in the embedding space to construct a query-conditioned page-level similarity matrix that highlights query-relevant semantics and dynamically determines the number of pages to retrieve. Across multiple encoders and LVLMs, ViSAR retrieves compact, query-adapted page sets that reduce RAG latency by up to 58.7\%, while maintaining or improving answer accuracy compared with fixed top-$k$ and adaptive retrieval heuristics. Furthermore, we show that the similarity matrix structure correlates with answer accuracy, suggesting future directions for retrieval quality-aware document understanding.

</details>


### [30] [Training seeds and model-selection stability in recommender-system evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02499)
*Juan Manuel Rodriguez,Oleg Lesota,Antonela Tommasel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推荐系统实验中单一训练随机种子假设的可靠性，通过固定数据划分、变化训练种子，从用户级指标敏感性、基于验证的模型选择和推荐列表一致性三个层面分析种子效应，发现种子变化的影响常可被检测到，建议将训练种子视为评估协议的一部分而非实现噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统实验往往依赖单一随机训练种子，假设运行间的随机性对评估结论影响有限。然而训练种子可能影响参数初始化、mini-batch排序、dropout、掩码、潜在采样和训练期负采样等多种算法相关机制，这种假设存在风险，需要系统验证。

Method: 保留数据划分不变，在多个超参数配置下改变训练种子，从三个层面分析种子效应：(1) 用户级指标敏感性；(2) 基于验证的模型选择；(3) 推荐列表一致性。

Result: 种子变化的影响在许多情况下可被检测到。其影响程度取决于配置间是否清晰分离、验证结果能否迁移到测试集，以及相似分数是否产生相似的top-k列表。

Conclusion: 报告单次种子实验结果可能夸大推荐系统评估的稳定性，训练种子应被视为评估协议的一部分而非偶然的实现噪声。

Abstract: Recommender-system experiments often rely on a single random training seed, assuming that run-to-run stochasticity has limited impact on evaluation conclusions. This assumption is risky, as a training seed may influence several algorithm-dependent mechanisms, including parameter initialization, mini-batch ordering, dropout, masking, latent sampling, and training-time negative sampling. We examine this assumption by fixing the data partition and varying the training seed across hyperparameter configurations. We analyze seed effects at three levels: user-level metric sensitivity, validation-based model selection and recommendation-list agreement. Results show that seed variation is often detectable. Its impact depends on whether configurations are clearly separated, whether validation results transfer to test, and whether similar scores lead to similar top-$k$ lists. Findings suggest that reporting single-seed results can overstate the stability of recommender system evaluation, and that training seeds should be treated as part of the evaluation protocol rather than as incidental implementation noise.

</details>


### [31] [Recommender System as Slow and Fast Thinkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02671)
*Zichen Yuan,Xiaoxuan Dong,Linkun Dai,Jinwei Yang,Jining Luan,Dexu Yu,Chunxiao Li,Joemon M. Jose,Youhua Li,Hanwen Du,Junchen Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DS-Frame，一个自适应快慢推理框架，用于序列推荐系统。该框架包含快速系统处理常规预测、慢性系统进行迭代潜在状态精炼，以及一个可学习的选择器在可控计算预算下路由每个样本。在五个真实数据集上，DS-Frame一致性地提升了代表性子序列推荐骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态单次前向的序列推荐模型在常见行为模式上表现良好，但在具有挑战性的用户群体（如历史更长或物品画像非主流的用户）上性能显著下降。不同异构用户环境下的推荐效果差异较大，需要一个能够自适应分配计算资源的推理机制。

Method: DS-Frame由三个核心组件构成：(1) Fast System，用于高效的常规预测；(2) Slow System，用于迭代式的潜在状态精炼；(3) 一个可学习的路由器/选择器，在可控的计算预算下将每个样本路由到合适的系统路径上。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，DS-Frame一致性地提升了代表性序列推荐骨干模型的性能，在具有挑战性的用户子组上取得了更大的增益，并实现了有效的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应推理（adaptive inference）为构建更高效且更鲁棒的推荐系统提供了重要潜力，能够在不显著增加计算成本的情况下针对困难样本分配更多推理资源。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models are foundational to modern personalized services, yet their effectiveness varies substantially across heterogeneous user environments. In particular, static one-pass recommenders often perform well on common behavior patterns but degrade on operationally challenging user groups, such as users with longer histories or less mainstream item profiles. To address this limitation, we propose \textsc{DS-Frame}, an adaptive fast--slow inference framework for sequential recommendation. \textsc{DS-Frame} combines a Fast System for efficient routine prediction, a Slow System for iterative latent refinement, and a learned selector that routes each sample under a controllable computation budget. Experiments on five real-world datasets show that \textsc{DS-Frame} consistently improves representative sequential recommendation backbones, with larger gains on challenging groups and effective accuracy--efficiency trade-offs. This highlights the potential of adaptive inference for more efficient and robust recommendation. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/ZichenYuan233/Recommender-System-as-Slow-and-Fast-Thinkers}{this link}.

</details>


### [32] [Incremental Pooled LLM Evaluation for Cost-Effective Retrieval Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02745)
*Max Nelson,Hanoz Bhathena,Aviral Joshi,Saket Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"池化LLM评估"方法用于生产环境RAG系统的检索模型选择：由LLM对当前候选系统检索结果的并集进行判断，新系统加入时仅判断其新增文档，复用已有判断结果。在4个检索基准（11个系统）上验证与金标准评估高度相关，97%的成对系统排序在qrels自举不确定性范围内保持不变；生产部署中65-80%的判断可复用，评估成本降低至4.9倍以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统需要可靠的对比评估来选择检索模型，但大规模获取相关性标注成本高昂且难以随新候选系统加入而重复进行。

Method: 提出池化LLM评估工作流：LLM对当前候选系统检索结果并集进行判断形成"池"，新系统加入时仅对增量新文档进行判断，所有系统基于相同的判断集合进行比较。在4个检索基准上以11个系统（密集、稀疏、混合配置）验证，并在金融新闻QA系统中部署比较62种检索配置。

Result: 池化LLM评估排名与金标准评估高度相关；97%成对系统排序在qrels自举不确定性下保持一致；生产中文档重叠带来65-80%判断复用，最多降低4.9倍评估成本。

Conclusion: 池化LLM评估是生产环境中增量检索模型选择的一种实用且成本效益高的工作流，无需对已评估文档重新标注即可基准测试新检索候选系统。

Abstract: Selecting a retrieval model for a production RAG system requires reliable comparative evaluation, but obtaining relevance judgments at scale is expensive and difficult to repeat as new candidate systems arrive. We study pooled LLM evaluation, in which an LLM judges the union of documents retrieved by the current set of candidate systems, and the pool is then expanded incrementally as new systems are introduced by judging only the new documents they contribute. These judgments are reused to evaluate all systems on a common basis. We validate this approach on four retrieval benchmarks with 11 systems spanning dense, sparse, and hybrid configurations, and deploy it to compare 62 retrieval configurations for a financial news QA system. Pooled LLM rankings correlate strongly with gold-standard evaluation across datasets, and 97% of pairwise system orderings are preserved once bootstrap uncertainty in the qrels is taken into account. In production, document overlap yields 65-80% judgment reuse and up to 4.9x lower evaluation cost, allowing teams to benchmark new retrieval candidates without re-judging previously assessed documents. These results suggest pooled LLM evaluation is a practical and cost-effective workflow for incremental retrieval model selection in deployed systems.

</details>
