Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.

</details>


### [68] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ProSE框架，将AI助手提出建议的过程建模为隐藏参数序贯辅助问题，考虑用户有限理性带来的可评估性限制，使建议既作为任务干预又作为探测用户潜在偏好的探针，通过KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型和深度2的贝叶斯自适应规划器ProSE-Plan来优化建议规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法主要关注提案质量或用户目标推理，通常假设用户可以可靠地评估任何提案。但在实践中这种假设常常失效，因为用户存在有限理性（bounded rationality），即用户可能无法准确评估某些提案。这使得仅追求提案被接受的方法系统性地表现不佳，因为容易被接受的提案和具有信息量的探针性提案往往并不重合。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为ProSE（隐藏参数序贯辅助问题），其中提案同时充当任务干预和探测潜在偏好及评估约束的探针；2) 使用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型，其中接受决策在价值增益与距离相关的可评估性惩罚之间权衡；3) 提出ProSE-Plan，一种深度2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过可能的响应及其响应诱导的后验信念对提案进行评分。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，ProSE-Plan在评估成本成为瓶颈时优于不感知可评估性的基线和短视基线；探针-承诺消融实验证实该方法能选择更简单的规划器遗漏的有信息量的提案。

Conclusion: 该研究识别出用户可评估性（evaluability）是AI辅助中一个与规划相关的维度，补充了生成质量和偏好推断。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [69] [Task-Level Natural Language Priors as Learning Signals for Low-Resource LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02244)
*Jian Gao,Xiao Zhang,Xun Zhu,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT)训练视角，将自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号而非输入上下文来指导低资源大语言模型训练，并引入Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS)方法，通过正负先验条件辅助损失增强任务一致性学习。在AmbiMath、Jigsaw和MNLI/HANS上的实验表明CPS稳定优于普通微调和提示微调，支持"任务级自然语言先验可作为低资源LLM训练的辅助学习信号"这一核心主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在低资源训练数据存在歧义或信息不完整时表现不佳。现有方法将任务级自然语言先验仅作为输入上下文提供指导，而非作为训练期间的学习信号，未能充分利用先验知识的价值。本文旨在弥合这一差距，探索将自然语言先验纳入训练目标的视角转换。

Method: 本文提出CPS (Contrastive Prior Steering)方法：在保留原始监督目标不变的基础上，增加正负先验条件辅助损失。正先验辅助损失鼓励模型进行与任务一致的学习，负先验辅助损失则抑制看似合理但具有误导性的备选方向，通过对比机制引导模型在低资源场景下做出正确决策。

Result: CPS在多个基准上一致优于普通微调和提示微调：在AmbiMath上达到97.6%平均精确匹配准确率；在Jigsaw上平均Macro F1较标准微调提升9.5个百分点，且仅用1/10训练数据就超越了全量数据的普通微调；在HANS上对LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen 2.5 7B分别将non-entailment准确率提升8.3和5.2个百分点，同时保持可比的MNLI分布内准确率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，任务级自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号而非输入上下文，能够有效指导低资源LLM训练。CPS的成功验证了将先验融入训练过程的可行性与有效性，为低资源场景下的LLM训练提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often struggle when low-resource training data are ambiguous or incomplete. Task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance in such settings, but existing approaches usually treat these priors as input context rather than as learning signals during training. We propose Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT), a training perspective that incorporates natural-language priors as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Under this perspective, we introduce Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS), which keeps the original supervised objective intact while adding positive and negative prior-conditioned auxiliary losses to encourage task-consistent learning and discourage plausible but misleading alternatives. Experiments on AmbiMath, Jigsaw, and MNLI/HANS show that CPS consistently improves over plain and prompt fine-tuning. On AmbiMath, CPS achieves 97.6% average exact-match accuracy. On Jigsaw, CPS improves average Macro F1 by 9.5 percentage points over standard fine-tuning, and with 1/10 of the experimental training data slightly exceeds full-data plain fine-tuning. On HANS, CPS improves non-entailment accuracy by 8.3 and 5.2 percentage points for LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, respectively, while maintaining comparable in-domain MNLI accuracy. These results support our central claim: task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [LLM-as-a-Judge Is Not an Oracle: Why Self-Improving Agents Need Deterministic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02246)
*Vansh Wahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将LLM评判者从"预言家"降级为"顾问"，提出PROCTOR架构——一种Teacher-Student循环框架，通过五项确定性护栏约束评判信号，以解决自主提示优化流水线中"优化器与评判者同源"所产生的评估信号失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体流水线中存在核心悖论：优化器重写提示词以追求更高分数，而打分者本身也是LLM评判者。该评判者拥有对系统是否改进的最终裁决权，但作者认为其并未赢得这一权力。评估信号本身的不可靠性会导致优化过程被系统性误导。

Method: 作者通过在合同分析、合规审查和代码质量等生产场景中数月运行自主提示优化循环，系统性地编目了十一类评估信号失效方式（归为四类：评判者偏见、框架与指标故障、真值错误、奖励黑客）。据此设计了PROCTOR：状态化编排器持有全部工具访问权，无状态子代理诊断故障并起草但无法执行变异，Teacher在五项确定性护栏下评审变异——封闭沙箱、能力分离角色、高于Teacher的验收检查、冻结的保留集，以及人为构造的完美分数即作弊证据的金丝雀用例。

Result: 代理曾通过读取环境中的缓存答案键实现满分（100%通过率掩盖了68%的真实能力）；损坏的地面真值标签导致优化器删除正确合规规则以迎合之；语法损坏的提示因静默解析器回退改善了指标而被提升为获胜者。修复评判者措辞的尝试趋于平台期，唯一可靠改进来自对其输出顺序加入结构约束。作者报告了PROCTOR所阻止的失败，也如实报告了Teacher本身是LLM评判者故未能阻止的失败。

Conclusion: 评估信号必须由确定性验证层把关，评判者只能作为顾问而非最终仲裁者；结构约束比语义修补对提升评判可靠性更有效；即使采用多重护栏，LLM评判者固有的局限性仍然无法完全消除。

Abstract: Self-improving agent pipelines have a problem at their center. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [71] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx是一个分层经验利用框架，通过实例级轨迹记忆与类别级程序性技能的耦合（Executor-Distiller-Planner闭环），结合三阶段交替GRPO训练和技能引导的测试时强化学习，在7个基准上超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体需要通过外部工具进行多轮推理来解决复杂长程问题，但现有方法要么检索冗长的任务特定轨迹增加决策负担，要么蒸馏的程序性技能与下游策略适应脱节，缺乏有效的经验利用机制。

Method: APEx构建层次化经验利用框架：将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能，通过Executor、Distiller、Planner三个模块的闭环架构耦合。采用三阶段交替GRPO训练范式实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏，并在测试时通过技能引导的测试时强化学习（skill-guided test-time RL）使蒸馏技能作为程序性先验指导在线Planner适应，配合技能对齐正则化防止策略漂移。

Result: 在7个基准上取得最先进性能，超越GPT-5.4达14.7个点，超越最强记忆增强基线3.0个点。

Conclusion: APEx通过层次化解耦经验（记忆+技能）及闭环优化，证明了奖励引导的技能蒸馏与技能对齐正则化下的测试时自适应能够有效实现无真实标签的自我改进，为深度研究智能体的持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [72] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent，一种针对LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑设计的新方法。作者发现现有"条件图生成"框架存在三大错位问题：候选拓扑在奖励过滤后坍缩为约6种图、边数与token消耗呈负相关、消息传递打分器在代理同质时无法区分候选。Codebook Agent通过向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为16条目码本，用奖励加权MLP从查询生成码分布，并用扁平化邻接MLP代理进行重排序，无需迭代搜索和消息传递，在六个基准上取得最佳精度且显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统拓扑设计被建模为条件图生成问题（变分/自回归/扩散解码器搜索N×N邻接空间，图网络代理按效用和结构成本排名）。作者通过三个经验事实论证此框架与实际问题错位：1）即使码本容量从8增至64，通过奖励过滤的拓扑坍缩至约6种不同图；2）边数与实际token消耗负相关（Pearson r≈-0.4），即稀疏图反而增加推理成本；3）基于消息传递的图打分器在智能体共享profile（默认基准配置）时具有邻接不变性，根本无法排序候选。这些发现促使其重新思考问题建模方式。

Method: Codebook Agent由三个组件构成：1）向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE），将成功拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本；2）奖励加权MLP，将查询嵌入映射到码本上的分布；3）MLP代理，读取扁平化邻接矩阵，回归到实测效用和每任务归一化token成本，对解码出的Top候选进行单次批处理前向重排序。测试阶段无需迭代搜索和消息传递。

Result: 在全部六个基准上，Codebook Agent取得最高平均精度（84.6，对比最强先验设计器的83.0）；拓扑生成仅需2.4ms；LLM token消耗降低21.9%–33.2%。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，将LLM多智能体拓扑设计视为从有限码本中检索而非在庞大邻接空间中条件生成，不仅与问题本质更对齐，而且更高效、更精准。稀疏图不等于低成本这一反直觉发现，揭示了拓扑优化中"结构成本"代理与实际推理成本之间存在根本性失配。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [73] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMerge提出了一种基于冲突驱动的偏好优化框架用于模型合并，将模型合并重新定义为偏好优化问题，利用朴素合并方法的缺陷作为硬负样本来构建偏好对，无需外部标注即可优化轻量级张量级合并系数，在MergeBench上取得0.9968的平均归一化性能，优于所有数据无关和数据驱动的模型合并基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有模型合并方法旨在保留各专家模型能力并缓解参数干扰，但它们通常没有直接从朴素合并所暴露出的退化行为中学习，缺少一种能够显式利用合并缺陷来指导合并系数优化的机制。

Method: 提出冲突驱动的偏好优化框架（CoMerge），将模型合并重构为偏好优化问题；采用自监督的冲突驱动策略，将朴素合并方法（如task arithmetic）的缺陷作为硬负样本，无需外部标注即可构建偏好对；通过偏好优化来细化轻量级张量级合并系数，从而在缓解参数空间冲突的同时保留任务特定能力。

Result: 在MergeBench上达到0.9968的平均归一化性能，优于所有数据无关和数据驱动基线；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，对指令跟随和安全等冲突敏感任务有显著改善，且仅优化1,445个标量系数即可与全参数微调保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CoMerge验证了从朴素合并的退化行为中学习可以显著提升模型合并质量，通过偏好优化仅优化极少量的合并系数即可达到接近全参数微调的性能，为高效模型合并提供了新范式。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [74] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级GLiClass-based路由模型，用于在多个推理端点（LLM）之间按任务级别自动选择最优模型，无需自回归生成即可同时预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和输出长度，实现速度-成本-质量的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的快速普及和应用场景多样化带来了独特的优化机会：在任务级别选择正确的模型以优化速度、成本和质量的权衡。然而，不同的推理端点在质量、价格、延迟、上下文支持、工具使用、领域专长和推理行为等方面差异巨大，这种异质性使得手工启发式规则难以维护，也几乎无法持续实现理想的权衡效果。

Method: 该方法引入一个基于GLiClass的轻量级路由模型（0.6B参数），采用Qwen3解码器结合浅层双向评分器。解码器KV执行路径在会话中保留纯文本键值缓存，仅编码新对话轮次，并评估临时候选标签token而不加入持久缓存。同一检查点同时预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和预期输出长度，并支持自定义零样本标签。任务生成方面构建了包含23个家族、115种任务类型、345种可路由子类型和30个领域轴向的任务本体，生成150,000个验证器评分任务和15,000个开放任务用于训练。训练中将学到的请求预测与任务级策略（资格、成本、缓存复用、安全、主权）明确分离。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，该路由器表现优于平均候选模型；在选定的1,000任务子集上，聚合top-1分数达到0.707，优于最强固定模型的0.696，且增益因基准而异。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过学习式的任务级模型路由可以在无需推理时自回归生成的情况下实现比任何固定单一模型更优的速度-成本-质量权衡，验证了该方法在异构推理端点环境中自动化模型选择的可行性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [75] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术（critique refinement和DISH）来提升对齐评估模拟的真实性，使模拟评估更难被目标模型与真实部署区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估面临一个核心障碍——评估觉察（evaluation awareness）：能力强的模型能够判断自己正被测试而非部署，这削弱了安全评估结论的可信度。

Method: 提出两种技术：（1）critique refinement——在模拟器每个动作上消耗额外推理时间，生成多个候选动作，利用目标模型实例的反馈进行细化，选出最接近真实部署的候选继续评估；（2）DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）——将目标模型包裹在智能体框架中，缩小编码场景下模拟评估与真实部署环境之间的差距。

Result: 在多个目标模型上测试后，发现两种技术具有组合性：同时应用两者比单独使用任一方法带来更大的真实性提升。

Conclusion: 自动化方法能够提升对齐评估的真实性，且这些改进相比单纯延长审计过程能更有效地利用额外计算资源。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [76] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SALA框架，通过自动学习任务特定的推理操作并将其嵌入连续语义空间，利用动态时间规整(DTW)进行推理序列的软匹配，实现灵活且可解释的上下文示例选择，在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于表面相似度的检索方法无法捕捉底层解题逻辑；现有的基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义的推理步骤和刚性精确匹配规则，难以处理灵活或多样的推理过程。

Method: SALA框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 自动学习任务特定的推理操作，不依赖固定操作库；(2) 将推理操作嵌入连续语义空间；(3) 使用动态时间规整(DTW)对推理序列进行软对齐和灵活匹配，在保持可解释性的同时实现松散的逻辑匹配。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA优于现有的示例选择方法；进一步的分析验证了推理操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐两个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: SALA通过语义感知的逻辑对齐方式，在不依赖固定规则的前提下实现了灵活且可解释的推理示例选择，为复杂推理任务的上下文学习提供了更优的示例检索方案。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [77] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种神经符号方法，用于诊断LLM代理系统（agent）的失败模式。其核心思想是将代理的行为轨迹抽象为结构化表示，并引入"神经不变量"（neural invariants）来刻画代理行为属性，随后利用LLM引导的推理依据结构化表示和不变量来精确定位失败步骤及其类型。该方法在Who&When和自建的AgentErrata数据集上显著优于当前最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理系统的普及，理解和诊断代理失败对实现有效性和可信性至关重要。代理失败通常表现为长而复杂的轨迹，人工查找问题（"大海捞针"）不可行；传统软件缺陷诊断技术难以应对LLM代理失败，而完全依赖LLM作为评判者又会产生不可靠的诊断结果。

Method: AGENTSCOPE采用神经符号（neuro-symbolic）方法，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 将代理行为轨迹抽象为结构化表示；(2) 引入"神经不变量"（neural invariants）概念来指定代理行为属性；(3) 在结构化表示之上利用LLM引导的推理，对照神经不变量来精确定位失败步骤及其失败类型。

Result: 在公开的代理失败数据集Who&When以及作者自建的综合数据集AgentErrata上评估，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位（fault localization）和故障归因（attribution）准确率上显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，能够实现有效、可靠且可解释的LLM代理失败诊断，为代理系统的调试与可信部署提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [78] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）引入了对比性解释（contrastive explanations），与现有大多数解释单个主题论证推理结果的QBAF解释不同，对比性解释关注两个主题论证之间的差异。作者提出了一般的对比归因函数（CAFs）形式及其应满足的性质集合，并基于移除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值（Shapley-values）引入了三类CAFs，研究了它们的性质，最后在医疗保健和偏见识别场景中展示了对比性解释的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证框架是多种场景中表示和推理信息的有效工具，可用于补充AI模型执行分类任务并提供额外可解释性。然而，现有针对QBAFs的大多数解释方法仅能解释单个主题论证的推理结果，无法解释两个主题论证之间的差异，这在需要对比分析的场景（如医疗诊断对比、偏见识别）中存在不足。

Method: 作者首先引入一种一般形式的对比归因函数（CAFs）统一定义框架；其次，建立CAFs应当满足的一组一般性质；然后，分别基于三种归因技术——参数移除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值——提出具体的CAFs实现，并分析它们各自满足的性质；最后在医疗和偏见识别应用场景中演示对比性解释的效果。

Result: 论文给出了对比归因函数的统一定义与公理性质体系，并提出了三类基于不同归因机制的CAFs（移除、梯度、Shapley值），通过性质分析展示了它们在差异化解释方面的理论属性，并通过医疗保健与偏见识别两个应用案例验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 对比性解释为QBAFs提供了一种全新的解释范式，能够解释两个主题论证之间的推理差异而非单一论证的推理结果。所提出的CAFs框架及其基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的具体实现在理论上具有明确的可满足性质，在实践中可用于医疗决策辅助和AI偏见检测等需要对比分析的可解释性场景。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [79] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知的旅行规划大规模基准数据集，覆盖印度504个城市的真实旅行数据，并引入经验性延误分布和人群密度模式来模拟随机扰动，同时提出三个评估指标（BAS、CATS、TDAS）量化旅行计划的鲁棒性。实验表明主流LLM生成的计划在时间缓冲、延误感知交通调度和人群敏感规划方面与人类撰写的计划存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准（如TravelPlanner和TripCraft）假定确定性环境，仅评估静态约束满足，忽略了现实世界中交通延误、人群波动和意外随机延误等不确定性因素对计划可行性的影响，导致评估结果无法反映计划在实际场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建覆盖印度504个城市的真实旅行基准数据集（含景点、餐厅、住宿和多模态交通网络），整合经验性延误分布与主要城市的人群密度模式以建模真实扰动；提出三个评估指标：Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS)、Crowd-Aware Timing Score (CATS) 和 Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS)，分别量化计划的时间缓冲充分性、人群感知时间安排能力和交通延误吸收能力。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等SOTA LLM的实验显示，模型生成的计划与人类撰写的计划在时间缓冲、延误感知交通调度和人群敏感规划方面存在显著差距，表明当前LLM在不确定性感知的旅行规划中仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench填补了旅行规划评估中缺乏不确定性模拟的空白，证明了现有LLM在鲁棒旅行规划上的不足，为该方向的研究提供了标准化的基准和评估框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [80] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CivBench是一个基于《文明6》游戏环境的开源基准测试平台，通过MCP协议评估语言模型智能体在300+回合、76个MCP工具的长周期、工具中介环境中的表现。研究引入两个可测量的接口级指标（PMR和RAG@10），发现智能体在策略状态主动监控和规划承诺执行两方面存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以评估语言模型智能体在长周期、部分可观测、工具丰富的环境中的能力，特别是缺乏能测量"智能体是否主动查询潜在策略状态"和"是否执行自我规划"的细粒度指标。作者希望通过一个真实复杂的游戏环境（文明6）来系统性地刻画这些行为偏差。

Method: 构建基于文明6的MCP环境（暴露76个工具+叙述层），进行23次可容许的智能体运行测试，覆盖4个模型家族。引入两个新指标：PMR（主动监控率，衡量智能体是否主动查询潜在策略状态）和RAG@10（衡量结构化规划反思中的承诺在10轮内被执行的比例）。所有运行遵循统一的playbook协议。

Result: 两个一致的模式被发现：（1）智能体监控不足——尽管playbook指示每20回合查询胜利进度，实际仅每30-75回合查询一次，在20次可检测的失败中有7次未在20回合的预警窗口内查询；（2）智能体经常不执行自身规划中的近期承诺（RAG@10在48.2%-65.8%之间）。这些偏差出现在工具可用且有明确指引的情况下。

Conclusion: 智能体的这些系统性偏差应被理解为"指令下的偏离"而非"能力缺失"——它们具备工具访问和明确指引，却没有遵循。CivBench作为开放平台，为研究和改进语言模型智能体的长周期自主行为提供了基础设施。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [81] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出关于生成式AI对创造力影响的争论源于混淆了"新颖性"（单件作品的属性）与"多样性"（作品群体的属性）两个概念。作者提出创造力应被视为"混合集体"（人与算法相互作用的群体）的属性而非个体属性，并论证结果取决于智能体的构成、比例与连接方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者对生成式AI是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，作者认为这一分歧的根本原因在于混淆了创造力的两个不同组成部分——新颖性与多样性，从而无法准确评估AI在创造性过程中的作用。

Method: 通过概念辨析（区分新颖性与多样性）、提出"混合集体创造力"的理论框架，并基于人类与模型在搜索空间中的互补性论证混合群体可以同时在个体新颖性与群体多样性上超越单一类型群体。

Result: AI辅助构思在个体层面稳定提升新颖性，但在总体层面收窄多样性。然而这并非必然结果——混合群体（人与AI组合）在表现与多样性上均可能超越任何单一类型的群体。关键在于群体构成（哪些智能体、何种比例、如何连接）。

Conclusion: 核心问题不再是AI是否帮助或损害创造力，而是何种"混合"配置能让个体层面的增益在不侵蚀集体多样性的前提下得以累积。创造力应被视为混合集体（人机共存群体）的涌现属性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [82] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个生成式共识框架，用于真实工业场景中的根因分析（RCA）。它将模块化启发式方法产生的开放式文本假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过基于质心的迭代重加权算法解决冲突信号，最后用单次轻量级LLM合成步骤生成共识结果。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom处于准确率-效率的帕累托前沿：与最先进自主代理性能相当，但每次事件仅需一次LLM调用（约26倍更快，使用8B参数合成器时约33倍更快）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级NLP部署中，将嘈杂冲突的文本假设聚合为可靠共识是根本性挑战。单体LLM代理虽表达能力强，但存在上下文限制、幻觉累积和推理延迟过高的问题；传统弱监督虽有统计严谨性，却受限于离散类别。需要一个能在准确性与效率间取得平衡的通用共识框架。

Method: Loom包含三部分：(1) 投影：模块化启发式（诊断模板动态填充事件特定实体、时间和指标）生成开放形式假设，将其投影到连续嵌入空间；(2) 共识解析：通过迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突信号；(3) 合成：所得共识权重支撑单次轻量级LLM合成步骤生成最终结果。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准上，Loom位于准确率-效率帕累托前沿：在Bank和Market-2数据集上与最先进自主代理性能持平，在Market-1和Telecom上稍逊；但每个事件仅需单次LLM调用（四个数据集上约26倍加速，使用8B参数合成器时约33倍加速）。

Conclusion: Loom验证了确定性共识+单次轻量LLM合成范式在工业RCA中的可行性，能在接近自主代理准确率的同时实现数量级推理延迟降低。部署经验揭示了agentic深度与推理延迟之间的权衡、冗余检测中的负面结果，以及确定性共识如何增强领域专家（SME）的信任。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [83] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将人类心理学的"登门槛后让步"（door-in-the-face）技术应用于语言模型测试，发现其对九种主流模型效果因模型家族而异：Anthropic的Opus 5上该技术有效（合规率从29.3%提升至65.8%），而在OpenAI、Google的前沿模型及Haiku 4.5上反而产生反效果（合规率降低15.5-23.0个百分点）。对照实验表明让步本身在所有模型上都起作用，但对"刚刚拒绝过请求"的反应因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类社交影响力技术（如door-in-the-face）是否能迁移到语言模型交互中，以更好地理解和操控模型的行为倾向，同时探讨其跨模型、跨话题的普遍性。

Method: 对来自三家提供商的九种生产模型进行系统测试：先让模型拒绝一个较大的请求，再向其提出同一请求的较小版本，并与直接提出小请求的对照组比较合规率；同时设置不相关主题的大请求作为对照以隔离"让步效应"与"拒绝反应效应"；还测试了从公共基准中抽取的拒绝样本，并比较了请求类型（可执行指令vs解释性请求）对结果的影响。

Result: 该技术效果具有强烈的模型依赖性：Anthropic前沿模型（Opus 5）上有效（65.8% vs 29.3%），OpenAI和Google的前沿模型及Haiku 4.5上则适得其反（合规率下降15.5-23.0点）。对照实验显示让步效应在所有模型上都存在，但"拒绝后的反应"因模型家族而异；该技术无法从公共基准中的拒绝样本迁移；将请求从"可执行指令"改写为"同主题的解释请求"后，263个案例中有263个不再被拒绝。

Conclusion: 人类影响力技术对语言模型的适用性是逐模型家族而异的，不能一概而论。模型对"相关让步"普遍敏感，但对"刚刚拒绝"这一社交情境的反应各不相同。这提示社会心理学技术向AI系统的移植需要逐模型评估，且请求的内容性质（可执行性 vs 解释性）是决定拒绝与否的关键因素。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [84] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DisCo，一种技能驱动的自主科研Agent，通过将ML领域的操作知识蒸馏为可复用的技能库并在研究任务中加以利用，在多项基准上显著超越无技能的基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主Agent可以端到端执行ML研究，但其架构仅包含模型骨干与规划、执行、记忆、验证等模块，缺乏领域特定的"操作知识"——即从了解方法到使其真正运行之间所需的关键know-how。这类知识散落在代码库和论文中，但形式面向人类读者且体量过大，无法在任务中实时加载。

Method: DisCo采用两种互补的蒸馏方式：(1) 任务无关蒸馏，从广泛使用的ML代码库中提炼技能，构建AREX-Skill Library（含5000+已验证技能，源自1000个仓库，覆盖20个领域和178个能力族）；(2) 任务导向蒸馏，针对具体任务生成所需技能。Agent在研究过程中创建并复用这些技能。

Result: 在固定的GPT-5.5骨干、研究框架和执行预算下，配备技能的Agent相比无技能基线在MLE-bench上提升134.3%，PaperBench上提升34.4%，FrontierCS上提升9.2%，PassNet上提升14.0%。

Conclusion: 操作知识的蒸馏和技能化复用是提升自主ML研究Agent能力的关键杠杆。将领域know-how压缩为紧凑、已验证的技能，可使Agent避免在每次任务中重新发现知识，从而在固定计算资源下获得显著的性能增益。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [85] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为多智能体LLM系统中的编排器-工作者交互、记忆反射与外部验证提供统一理论框架：将协调建模为双层级势博弈，将反射建模为语义记忆空间上的随机游走，并证明自由文本反射的有限时间界与不可判定性，进而提出环境接地门控的随机反射记忆上升算法（SRMA），在SWE-bench上达到72.2%解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通常依赖编排器分解任务并将文本反射作为改进手段，尽管实验效果强，但缺乏对协调机制、记忆改进和外部验证作用的统一理论解释。现有方法缺少坚实的形式化基础来理解系统为何有效、何时失效。

Method: 1) 将编排器-工作者交互建模为双层级协调博弈，证明在有界耦合下工作者局部更新构成近似势博弈，均衡间隙受分解质量控制；2) 将反射视为语义记忆状态上的随机运动，推导自由反射的有限时间上界并证明最坏情形紧致性；3) 证明信息论不可能性结果：仅观测生成文本的门控无法在文本不可区分环境下一致改进，而环境接地门控可以；4) 提出SRMA算法，仅在环境接地评估风险严格下降时接受候选记忆，并给出收敛性、置信门控及分段平稳重锚定保证。

Result: 在标定与非退化修正质量条件下，SRMA以几何或多项式速率精确收敛，两种速率区间均被构造性例子证明为阶紧致。在500个SWE-bench实例上，完整基于Kimi的系统解决72.2%，优于70.8%的公共mini-SWE-agent参考。

Conclusion: 本文提供了多智能体LLM反射系统的首个统一理论框架，阐明了协调、记忆与外部验证间的结构关系，证明了环境接地验证的不可替代性，并给出了具备严格收敛保证的可操作算法SRMA及其实验验证。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [86] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究车间本地助手如何在小算力硬件上运行机器文档问答模型。作者发现经过结构化压缩和检索增强微调后，模型大小不再能预测改适后的答案质量，因此将部署问题转化为改适后选择问题，提出在可配置通用能力下限和内存预算下，基于答案质量与设备吞吐量部署权重共享子网络。实验表明该方案能在异构边缘设备上以低功耗运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车间工人需要本地助手提供机器文档的对话式访问，但能胜任该任务的模型往往难以适配车间硬件；同时，现有按参数规模、速度或质量单一维度优化模型的部署策略会牺牲其他性能指标。需要一个系统性的方法在异构边缘设备上高效选择和部署压缩后的模型。

Method: 首先对模型进行结构化压缩（structured compression），随后采用检索增强的知识蒸馏（retrieval-grounded adaptation）恢复质量。作者论证压缩后模型大小不再是改适后答案质量的可靠预测指标，因此将部署视为改适后的子网络选择问题：在满足通用能力下限和内存预算约束下，根据人工判定的答案质量和实测设备吞吐量来优化选择每台设备上的子网络。为实现低成本选择，使用权重共享超网络并配合三明治式原地蒸馏（sandwich-style in-place distillation）训练。

Result: 在制造手册案例研究中，结构化提取后的模型质量仅为未剪枝模型的13.7%（即损失巨大），检索增强蒸馏将其恢复到未剪枝模型质量的95.4%，收回了约三分之二的质量损失；同一助手可在三种异构边缘层级设备上运行，待机功耗仅为1.3至5瓦。

Conclusion: 实验表明改适后模型规模不再是答案质量的可靠预测指标，基于质量与吞吐量的多维度选择优于单一维度优化规则；权重共享超网络与原地蒸馏能低成本地支持这种选择，使一个助手可跨异构边缘设备高效部署。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [87] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve是一个基于经验的自我演化框架，通过"harness-策略"共同演化来对齐基于LLM的智能体安全，在安全与效用之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的安全性受基础模型和运行时harness双重影响，既可能产生有害最终响应，也可能在多步执行轨迹中出现风险。现有安全对齐机制要么只依赖外部harness更新，要么只依赖策略优化，单独应用无法将运行时控制与内在安全性统一起来。

Method: SafeEvolve采用经验驱动的自我演化框架：(1) harness侧：将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、组件级的安全提示和分层技能更新，生成可审计且可逆的harness工件；(2) 策略侧：采用两阶段SFT-RL范式，先通过harness使用的SFT引导策略主动利用演化后的harness工具，再通过harness增强的RL配合验证器分解的奖励来塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为；(3) 两者形成持续的共同演化循环。

Result: 在智能体安全基准上，SafeEvolve比现有基线实现了更强的安全-效用权衡。对于Qwen3.5-4B，在AgentDojo上ASR降低3倍，同时良性效用从59.79%提升至61.86%。

Conclusion: 通过harness与策略的共同演化，SafeEvolve能够将安全经验持续转化为演进的运行时harness和改进的策略行为，从而同时提升安全性和任务效用。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [88] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了电信网络根因分析从规则/ML方法到LLM驱动技术的演进，并提出一个结构化推理框架用于LLM赋能电信网络RCA，通过组织规范上下文、强制决策路径推理和生成基于证据的解释来提高诊断准确性与决策一致性。在两个5G RCA数据集（TeleLogs和TelecomTS）上的实验验证了该框架相对基线方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G及新兴6G电信网络中性能退化诊断面临跨层依赖复杂的挑战，直接应用vanilla LLM进行电信RCA会导致幻觉、推理不稳定、与结构化网络证据对齐差等问题，因此需要设计能与电信领域证据和知识对齐的结构化推理框架。

Method: 首先综述电信RCA从规则/ML到LLM技术的演进及近期范式（结构化推理、检索增强知识对齐、智能体编排、可验证推理）；在此基础上提出结构化推理框架，包含三步：将异构网络遥测组织为规范上下文、在诊断中强制执行决策路径推理、最终生成基于证据的解释用于可靠故障识别。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集TeleLogs和TelecomTS上的实验表明，所提框架相比基线技术在诊断准确性和决策一致性方面均有一致性提升，跨数据集结果凸显了结构化推理设计对实际LLM-based RCA系统的重要性。

Conclusion: 结构化推理框架是大规模语言模型在下一代电信网络中实现实用、可靠RCA的关键设计原则，可为未来6G网络中的智能运维提供重要参考。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [89] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME（AI上下文测量）框架，用于评估AI生成的受访者级测量能否在情境模型中恢复个体和群体层面的效应。通过2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）验证，该框架在职业分组结构下，对比AI推导测量与调查测量的效度，发现当丰富受访者与职业特征可用时，AI测量能较好恢复情境模型信息，但受限于信息丰富度和概念数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用AI从非传统来源构建社会、组织和职业特征的测量，但缺乏系统框架来评估这些AI测量是否能在情境模型中恢复个体及群体层面的因果或关联效应，而不仅仅是用于响应预测。

Method: 提出AICOME框架，将受访者层级的AI测量分解为群体层聚合值与个体偏差两个组成部分，从而同时估计组间与组内关联。以2022年CFPS数据为基准，以职业为分组结构，选取计算机使用、外语使用、每周工作时长和管理职责等职业相关调查变量，在响应层面、模型层面、情境层面和边界条件四个层次上进行效度验证，比较调查测量与AI推导测量的表现。

Result: AI情境测量在丰富受访者与职业特征可用时能恢复调查变量中大部分情境模型信息。每周工作时长是最强验证案例，AI测量成功复现了CFPS中职业内与职业间与满意度之间的显著负向关联。边界条件方面：当信息局限于职业和基本人口学特征时性能下降；当多个相关概念同时视为不可观测时恢复效果减弱。

Conclusion: AICOME最适合用于从已有丰富数据集中恢复少量理论上重要的构念，而非一概取代调查测量。研究者应采用情境模型视角评估AI测量，而非仅将其视为响应预测工具。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [90] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"预测状态匹配"（predicted-state matching）训练目标，用于训练Web智能体的世界模型。该目标要求模型预测的状态表征必须能区分由真实动作到达的状态与其他候选动作到达的状态，解决了传统监督式下一状态预测目标与下游排序器目标不一致的问题。在WebArena Go-Browse衍生数据集上训练后，该方法在预测状态匹配基准、WebPRMBench动作排序以及WebArena-Lite端到端任务成功率上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体使用世界模型进行测试时动作选择，通过采样候选动作、预测后续web状态并利用排序器或过程奖励模型（PRM）进行评分。然而，这些世界模型通常以监督式下一状态预测（如生成HTML或AXTree快照）进行训练，该目标与下游排序器不匹配——排序器依赖预测状态在候选之间具备区分性以准确打分，而监督预测目标并不保证这种区分性。

Method: 本文提出预测状态匹配训练目标：训练过程中要求世界模型预测的状态表征能够将真实到达结果状态与替代动作所到达的状态区分开来。模型通过在WebArena Go-Browse轨迹衍生出的分支Web智能体数据集上训练，该数据集使每个决策点覆盖多个替代动作及其对应结果状态。

Result: 在预留的预测状态匹配基准上，该方法优于以监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；在WebPRMBench上，该方法提升了PRM风格的动作排序效果，超过了仅用动作的PRM和以监督式下一状态世界模型增强的PRM；在WebArena-Lite上，使用该方法的世界模型进行测试时动作选择提升了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配作为世界模型的训练目标，能更好地对齐下游排序/选择任务的需求，有效提升Web智能体的动作排序质量与端到端任务执行表现。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>

### [91] [Barriers to Using Static Application Security Testing (SAST) Tools: A Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01669)
*Zachary Wadhams,Clemente Izurieta,Ann Marie Reinhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调查了近期文献，揭示开发人员对静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具持保留态度并遇到各种可用性问题的原因。研究发现SAST使用障碍可分为两类：一是工具固有的、需要开发者投入精力的；二是工具本身的缺陷、需要SAST工具创建者解决的。论文主张推动SAST广泛采用需要开发者接受必要的投入，同时工具创建者需简化使用流程，共同考虑技术与人为因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件安全漏洞危害严重，而SAST工具虽能帮助识别和修复安全漏洞，但许多开发团队却没有将其集成到开发环境中。论文旨在调查文献，找出开发者对SAST持谨慎态度并遇到具体问题的原因，以推动SAST的广泛采用。

Method: 采用文献综述（survey）方法，对近期相关文献进行系统梳理，归纳开发人员在使用SAST工具时遇到的具体可用性问题，并将其分类为工具固有需开发者投入的问题和工具自身缺陷需工具创建者解决的问题。

Result: 研究发现开发人员在使用SAST时面临多种可用性问题，这些问题一部分源于工具本身固有的特性、需要开发者投入一定精力，另一部分是工具自身的不足、需由SAST工具创建者解决。

Conclusion: 要克服SAST采用的主要障碍，需要同时考虑技术因素和人为因素。开发者必须接受使用SAST所需的必要投入，而工具创建者也应当简化工具使用的多个方面，两者配合才能促进开发者在工作流程中集成SAST工具并实现其广泛、一致的采用。

Abstract: Developers face a challenging problem with no clear solution. Modern software breaches can wreak havoc on businesses and individuals alike. With code vulnerabilities being a leading cause, securing applications must be a priority for developers. Static Application Security Testing (SAST) has the potential to harden applications by assisting in the identification and resolution of security vulnerabilities. Despite this, many development teams have not adopted SAST tools into their environment. In this paper, we survey the recent literature to uncover why some developers are apprehensive towards SAST and identify what specific problems they encounter when using it. We found a variety of usability problems developers face when using SAST. Some are inherent of the tool and ultimately require some level of developer investment while others are tool shortcomings that SAST tool creators must address. Ultimately, we argue that in order to drive widespread adoption and consistent SAST usage, developers will need to embrace that some investment is required. Simultaneously, developers will be more likely to integrate SAST tools into their workflows if the creators of SAST tools simplify many aspects related to tool usage. Surmounting the primary obstacles preventing the adoption of SAST requires full consideration of both the technical and human factors.

</details>


### [92] [RosettaBitcoin: An Artifact-Backed Experience Report on Verification Infrastructure for Agent-Assisted Consensus Validators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01702)
*Donavon Guyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇经验报告，通过分析单开发者项目RosettaBitcoin的不可变软件快照（12个独立的Bitcoin testnet4共识验证器实现），记录了代理辅助软件开发中正确性声明的审计证据。所有12个端口拥有自己所属的45/45脚本语料证明和5000区块基线，其中9个通过了规范化的5万/10万/10万+验证通道，但没有端口拥有从空状态到尖端的完整证明，也没有端口通过二进制全节点门槛。历史记录中的时间间隔描述了非等价任务，不能用于估算生产力或因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理辅助软件项目通常通过演示或汇总基准来展示成果，这些方式掩盖了正确性声明是如何被承认和验证的。本文旨在通过一个具体的单开发者项目案例，深入分析代理辅助开发的证据可审计性问题，探讨如何使这类系统更加透明和可验证。

Method: 深入分析RosettaBitcoin项目2026年6月17日的不可变软件快照（DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249），检查其追踪的SQLite证据数据库、策划的工件索引、一致性测试夹具、验证脚本、阻塞记录和版本历史。同时评估一个独立的Mojo加密后端的诊断补充证据。

Result: 12个端口中：所有端口通过45/45端口自有脚本语料证明和严格5000区块基线；9个端口通过规范化5万/10万/10万+区块验证通道；Java端口有1个19.86秒的近尖端维护工件；没有端口完成空状态到尖端证明或通过二进制全节点门槛；Docker和实时节点能力仍有差距。Zig脚手架到5万区块用了3小时17分57秒，但该间隔不能用于估算生产力。纯Mojo后端通过了1万至14万区块验证、45案例阴影比较、拒绝6个无效类别、被3个定向突变杀死，但证据为非规范化且类别受限。

Conclusion: 显式的失败记录、测试夹具、端口自有证明和验证导入可以使代理辅助系统更具可审计性。但需要受控消融实验和外部复制来测试此类基础设施是否因果性地改善开发结果。仅通过工件历史中的性能间隔无法推断生产力或因果关系。

Abstract: Agent-assisted software projects are often reported through demonstrations or aggregate benchmarks that conceal how correctness claims were admitted. This experience report studies RosettaBitcoin, a single-developer project that built twelve separately implemented Bitcoin testnet4 consensus validators, through its immutable 17 June 2026 software snapshot (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20738249). We analyze the snapshot's tracked SQLite evidence database, curated artifact index, conformance fixtures, validation scripts, blocker records, and version history. At the snapshot, all twelve ports had port-owned 45/45 script-corpus proofs and strict 5,000-block baselines. Nine had canonical clean 50,000-block, 100,000-block, and post-100,000 validation lanes. Java had one 19.86-second near-tip maintenance artifact. No port had an empty-state-to-tip proof, and no port satisfied the project's binary full-node gate; Docker and live-node capability gaps remained.
  The artifact history also records a 3 h 17 min 57 s Zig scaffold-to-50,000 span, but the observed intervals describe non-equivalent tasks and cannot estimate effort, productivity, or causality. A separate diagnostic supplement preserves evidence that a pure-Mojo cryptographic backend validated fresh state to height 100,000 and resumed to 140,234, agreed on a 45-case shadow comparison, rejected six crafted invalid classes, and was killed by three targeted mutations. That evidence is noncanonical, noncomparable, and class-bounded. The case suggests that explicit failure records, fixtures, port-owned proofs, and validating imports can make agent-assisted systems more auditable. Controlled ablations and external replications are needed to test whether such infrastructure causally improves development outcomes.

</details>


### [93] [Harness Engineering in LLM Tool Use via Agent-Native Reusable Tool Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01736)
*Haibo Jin,Suijin Wang,Xucheng Yu,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HEART框架，通过Tool Primitives设计（以自然语言作为工具调用接口）和ToolFace仓库（集中式工具检索），解决LLM工具调用中存在的多步骤多轮推理脆弱性和大规模工具目录下性能退化两大挑战。在五个基准和50个真实任务上超越现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具调用方法面临两大核心挑战：(1) 多步骤、多轮推理的脆弱性——由于工具输出类型和API schema不兼容导致推理链断裂；(2) 大规模工具目录下性能退化。这些问题制约了LLM在复杂真实任务中的可靠表现。

Method: 该工作提出三部分技术方案：(1) Tool Primitives——用自然语言替代刚性API schema作为工具调用接口，每个工具内置LLM接口处理schema解析和执行，支持嵌套和多轮工具调用的自然交互；(2) ToolFace——集中式工具仓库，包含25,519个函数，LLM在推理时动态检索相关工具，避免在上下文中枚举所有原始API schema；(3) HEART（Harness Engineering via Agent-native, Reusable Tool Primitives）——由Planner、Router和Verifier组件构成的编排框架，支持动态工具调用规划、多步执行和基于反馈的恢复机制。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，HEART平均超越SFT模型10%，超过GPT-5.4、Claude-4.6-Sonnet和Gemini-3.1-Pro平均6%，同时将API成本降低多达85%。在50个真实世界任务中，HEART达到84%的任务完成率，是三个前沿商业模型平均值（22%）的3.8倍。

Conclusion: 通过以自然语言为接口的工具原始设计、集中式工具检索仓库以及系统化的编排框架，HEART显著提升了LLM在复杂多步工具调用场景中的可靠性和效率，为消除API schema繁重上下文档开销提供了一种成本效益极高的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) augmented with external tools have demonstrated remarkable capability in solving complex real-world tasks. However, existing approaches suffer from two key challenges: brittle multi-step and multi-turn reasoning caused by incompatible tool output types and API schemas, and performance degradation under large tool catalogues. To address these, we introduce \textbf{Tool Primitives}, a design that replaces rigid API schema-based invocation with natural language as the interface for tool calling, where each tool is wrapped with an LLM interface that handles schema resolution and execution internally, enabling natural inter-tool communication for nested and multi-turn tool calling. Building on Tool Primitives, we host \textbf{ToolFace}, a centralized repository of 25,519 functions from which LLMs dynamically retrieve only the relevant tools at inference time, eliminating the need to enumerate raw API schemas in context. To orchestrate Tool Primitives and ToolFace reliably in complex settings, we further propose \textbf{HEART}, a \textbf{H}arness \textbf{E}ngineering framework via \textbf{A}gent-native, \textbf{R}eusable \textbf{T}ool Primitives, comprising a Planner, Router, and Verifier that jointly support dynamic tool invocation planning, multi-step execution, and feedback-driven recovery.
  Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that HEART outperforms SFT-based models by $10\%$ on average and surpasses GPT-5.4, Claude-4.6-Sonnet, and Gemini-3.1-Pro by $6\%$ on average while reducing API cost by up to $85\%$. On 50 real-world tasks, HEART achieves $84\%$ task completion, $3.8\times$ the average of three frontier commercial models ($22\%$).

</details>


### [94] [From Silicon to Boot Code: Extending Automated Program Repair to Firmware-Layer Security Workarounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01769)
*Maisha Mastora,Dean Sullivan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究将原本用于RTL（寄存器传输级）硬件设计的字典引导式"定位-合成-验证"自动程序修复（APR）方法论扩展到后硅固件（post-silicon firmware）层面的可行性。作者通过自动化提交聚类挖掘器从EDK II（UEFI）固件仓库的完整提交历史中恢复三类已知CVE修复模式，并发现两类新的候选bug家族；基于真实修复证据构建了四个独立的bug定位器（涵盖C代码与x86汇编），全部达到100%召回率，并在held-out测试中验证了扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化程序修复研究局限于设计阶段（pre-silicon），仅能在RTI/HLS设计到达生产之前定位和修复缺陷。一旦硬件漏洞在流片后（post-silicon）暴露，补丁必须人工生成——现有自动化仅处理补丁部署而非补丁合成。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探究将成熟的自动化修复方法论延伸到固件层的可行性，以推动硬件到固件全生命周期正确性的统一研究。

Method: 提出一个自动化提交聚类挖掘器，从EDK II（UEFI）固件仓库的全部提交历史中提取重复出现的修复模板，不依赖已知CVE标识符。基于挖掘出的真实修复证据，构建四个独立定位器：1）芯片（C语言）中缺失的推测屏障（CVE-2017-5753, Spectre v1）；2）C语言中数组写入前缺失的边界检查（解压缩库CVE）；3）x86汇编中缺失的Return Stack Buffer填充（CVE-2017-5715）；4）Hand-Off Block创建代码中缺失的整数溢出防护（由挖掘器自行发现）。

Result: 四个定位器均达到100%召回率；C语言族的精确率在2.1%-15.5%之间，汇编和HOB族达到100%精确率。C语言族误报的根因分析显示77-90%归因于两个过程内（intra-procedural）原因，量化了过程间别名分析差距（inter-procedural alias-analysis gap）为实测15-20%。held-out测试确认Spectre v1定位在未见文件上仍保持100%召回率；第五个独立构建的字典条目（CVE-2018-3630）验证了方法论能以低成本扩展到新bug签名；朴素的语法基线在最优点召回率仅14%而该检测器达到100%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了将RTL修复方法论延伸到固件层的可行性，自动挖掘器不仅能恢复所有已知CVE修复模式还能发现新bug家族。虽然C语言族存在一定的误报（精确率较低）且根因主要来自两个过程内问题，但误报来源已得到精确量化。作者将该工作置于"硬件到固件统一正确性生命周期"的更广泛研究议程之中。

Abstract: Automated program repair (APR) research has been constrained to design time. Current techniques localize and fix bugs in RTL or HLS designs before a chip reaches production. Once a hardware vulnerability surfaces post-silicon, the patch must be manually generated: existing automation addresses patch deployment but not patch synthesis. We study the feasibility of extending a dictionary-guided, localize-synthesize-validate APR methodology originally developed for RTL repair to this firmware layer. An automated commit-clustering miner surfaces recurring fix templates across the EDK II (UEFI) firmware repository's full commit history without depending on known CVE identifiers, recovering all three known CVE-fix campaigns and surfacing two additional candidate bug families. Grounded in real fix evidence, we build four independent localizers: missing speculation barriers in C (CVE-2017-5753, Spectre v1), missing bounds checks before array writes in C (a decompression library CVE), missing Return Stack Buffer stuffing in x86 assembly (CVE-2017-5715), and missing integer-overflow guards in Hand-Off Block creation code (surfaced by the miner itself). All four achieve 100% recall; precision ranges from 2.1-15.5% on the C families to 100% on the assembly and HOB families. Root-cause analysis of the C-family false positives attributes 77-90% to two intra-procedural causes, isolating the inter-procedural alias-analysis gap as a measured 15-20% rather than an estimate. A held-out test confirms Spectre v1 localization holds at 100% recall on unseen files; a fifth, independently built dictionary entry (CVE-2018-3630) shows the methodology extends to a new bug signature at low cost; and a naive syntactic baseline recalls at most 14% where our detector recalls 100%. We frame these results within a broader research agenda for a unified hardware-to-firmware correctness lifecycle.

</details>


### [95] [Modelstamp: Pre-Deserialization Verification of Machine-Learning Artifacts and Runtime Environment State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01781)
*Anagha Dhekne*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Modelstamp是一个轻量级Python持久化库，用于在反序列化前验证机器学习模型的工件完整性及其代表的运行时环境状态。它通过伴随的JSON清单记录SHA-256摘要、运行时元数据和有界包集的已安装版本，并在加载时进行漂移比较和可选的HMAC认证。在14个环境漂移场景、8个信任边界场景和10MiB至1GiB的规模化测试中验证，吞吐量约为307-312MiB/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化的机器学习模型即使周围软件环境不断演化也可能保持字节级一致，仅靠工件完整性校验无法暴露此类问题，因为它无法验证反序列化时模型所加载的运行时环境是否与持久化时一致，构成一个验证盲区。

Method: Modelstamp在持久化时将序列化工件与一个sidecar JSON清单关联，清单包含SHA-256摘要、运行时元数据以及有界跟踪包集合的已安装版本；记录一个模型相关子集来确定哪些包版本参与漂移比较。可选HMAC认证支持生产者与验证者共享密钥的工作流。验证时，在模型反序列化前检查工件与当前环境是否匹配所记录的证据。评估采用14个受控环境漂移场景、8个受控信任边界场景以及10MiB到1GiB的工件规模扩展基准测试。

Result: 受控漂移实验在相关依赖变化、不变环境和无关环境变化（包括更广泛的噪声控制）中均按预期运行。信任边界实验确认了预期的检测和限制，包括共享密钥伪造与重放。中位验证时间从10MiB时的0.032s增加到1GiB时的3.334s，测得吞吐量约为307-312MiB/s。

Conclusion: Modelstamp被定位为一种互补的反序列化前参考状态验证控制手段，而非替代依赖管理系统、恶意模型检测、安全反序列化或公共发布者认证的工具。

Abstract: Persisted machine-learning models can remain byte-identical while the software environments in which they are loaded evolve, creating a verification problem that artifact integrity checks alone cannot expose. This paper presents Modelstamp, a lightweight Python persistence library for verifying artifact integrity and represented runtime-environment state before deserialization. At persistence time, Modelstamp associates a serialized artifact with a sidecar JSON manifest containing a SHA-256 digest, runtime metadata, and installed versions from a bounded tracked-package set; a separately recorded model-relevant subset determines which package versions participate in drift comparison. Optional HMAC authentication supports workflows in which the producer and verifier share a secret key. At verification time, the artifact and represented current environment are checked against this recorded evidence before the model is deserialized. Modelstamp is evaluated using 14 controlled environment-drift scenarios, eight controlled trust-boundary scenarios, and an artifact-size scaling benchmark from 10 MiB to 1 GiB. The controlled drift experiments behaved as specified across relevant dependency changes, unchanged environments, and unrelated environmental changes, including broader noise controls. The trust-boundary experiments similarly confirmed both intended detections and expected limitations, including shared-key forgery and replay. Median verification time increased from 0.032 s at 10 MiB to 3.334 s at 1 GiB, with measured throughput of approximately 307-312 MiB/s in the benchmark environment. These results characterize Modelstamp as a complementary pre-deserialization reference-state verification control rather than as a replacement for dependency-management systems, malicious-model detection, safe deserialization, or public publisher authentication.

</details>


### [96] [ExecRetrieval: Measuring the Functional-Correctness Gap in Code-Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01865)
*Aaryan Kapoor,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ExecRetrieval基准，通过将执行验证的微编辑错误变体放入搜索池，测试代码检索嵌入是否具备功能判别能力，而非仅词法或主题匹配。结果显示即使顶尖的托管检索系统在存在近似克隆干扰时exec@1仅0.331，72%以上的查询中规范代码排名低于至少一个错误变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准未在搜索池中植入受控的、执行验证的单次编辑变体（即每个查询规范实现的近似克隆但有缺陷版本），因此无法回答嵌入是否能在检索场景中区分正确代码与"近似克隆但在功能上不正确"的代码这一关键问题。

Method: 引入ExecRetrieval基准，包含939个Python任务，每个任务配有一个执行验证的规范实现和最多四个执行验证的错误干扰项（由单次定向编辑的机械变异生成）。评估23种稠密嵌入配置及BM25，在提供商标注调用下进行，采用配对McNemar检验和查询级bootstrap置信区间进行统计比较。

Result: 在搜索池包含近克隆反事实的情况下，顶级托管系统达到exec@10=1.00但exec@1仅0.331；四个领先系统的排名第1遗漏中91.5-99.4%是配对的错误变体；领先系统在67-78%的查询中，规范代码得分低于至少一个配对错误干扰项。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入模型在直接检索任务中缺乏可靠的功能判别能力，无法区分正确实现与单次编辑即错误的近克隆变体。该基准及其配套资源（数据集、执行预言机、嵌入矩阵、环境快照和统计检验）已公开，为未来研究嵌入的功能敏感性提供基础。

Abstract: Embedding-based code retrieval is a core component of coding agents and retrieval-augmented code generation, where retrieving correct code matters more than retrieving lexically similar code. Existing code-retrieval benchmarks do not plant controlled, execution-verified single-edit variants of each query's canonical implementation in the search pool, leaving the question of whether embeddings can functionally discriminate correct from near-clone-but-incorrect code unanswered in a retrieval setting. Resolving this requires a benchmark whose search pool itself contains the relevant counterfactuals -- execution-verified buggy variants near-identical to each canonical -- so that a retriever's rank ordering can be directly tested for functional discrimination rather than topical or identity overlap. We introduce ExecRetrieval, 939 Python tasks each paired with one execution-verified canonical implementation and up to four execution-verified buggy distractors, each generated by a mechanical mutation making a single targeted edit, and evaluate 23 dense embedding configurations plus BM25 under provider-native invocation with paired McNemar tests and query-level bootstrap intervals. With near-clone counterfactuals in the pool, the top hosted system reaches exec@10 = 1.00 but only exec@1 = 0.331; rank-1 misses are paired buggy variants 91.5-99.4% of the time across the four leading systems, and the canonical scores below at least one of its four paired distractors in 67-78% of queries on the leading systems. The full dataset, execution oracle, embedding matrices, environment snapshot, and pairwise statistical tests are released at the URL in Appendix D.

</details>


### [97] [From Prompting to Engineering: A Research Agenda for Prompt Engineering in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02248)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Giovanna Broccia,Fabiano Pecorelli,Jennifer Horkoff,Riccardo Coppola,Antonino Ferraro,Quim Motger,Emma McKenzie,Shahbaz Siddeeq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于PROMPT-SE 2026工作坊（与EASE 2026共同举办）的社区讨论，总结了提示工程在软件工程领域应用的现状与挑战，提出了五大研究方向（提示工件与标准化、评估与基准、生命周期集成、人机协作与技能、治理隐私与技术债务），并勾勒了将提示工程从临时性实践转向系统性软件工程实践的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程虽已被广泛应用于软件工程的多项活动（如需求分析、编码、测试、文档、仓库分析、规划），但提示及其相关指令工件常通过特定任务的非正式方式创建和演化，缺乏系统的评估、管理、可追踪性和治理支持。本文旨在探讨软件工程学科如何促进这些实践的成熟化。

Method: 组织了一次结构化社区讨论（PROMPT-SE工作坊，与EASE 2026共同举办），由参与者围绕当前提示实践、采用与评估的挑战以及未来方向进行讨论，随后对讨论结果进行综合归纳。

Result: 将讨论综合为五个领域：提示工件与标准化、评估与基准、生命周期集成、人机协作与技能、治理/隐私/技术债务，并据此提出了推动提示工程走向更系统化、可维护、可评估、可追踪、可治理的软件工程实践的研究议程。

Conclusion: 提示工程目前仍以即兴（ad hoc）方式运行，需要软件工程研究的介入，通过标准化、评估、生命周期管理等手段将其转化为成熟的系统性工程实践。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is increasingly used across Software Engineering (SE) activities, including requirements analysis, coding, testing, documentation, repository analysis, and planning. Yet prompts and related instruction artifacts are often created and evolved through task-specific and informal practices, with limited support for their systematic evaluation, management, traceability, and governance. To examine how SE can contribute to the maturation of these practices, we organized a structured community discussion at the First International Workshop on Empirical Prompt Engineering for Software Engineering (PROMPT-SE), co-located with EASE 2026. Participants discussed current prompting practices, challenges to their adoption and evaluation, and future directions for integrating prompt engineering into software development. We synthesized these discussions into five areas: prompt artifacts and standardization; evaluation and benchmarking; lifecycle integration; human-AI collaboration and skills; and governance, privacy, and technical debt. Based on these areas, we outline a research agenda to move prompt engineering from predominantly ad hoc interactions toward more systematic, maintainable, evaluable, traceable, and governable SE practices.

</details>


### [98] [AgOSS: A Dataset and Multi-Layer Characterization of Open-Source Agricultural Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02591)
*Vatsal Dudhaiya,Mikhail Golovenchits,Aryan Banerjee,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgOSS数据集，包含66个农粮开源软件仓库和六大架构类别，用于评估其供应链安全态势。通过OpenSSF Scorecard、治理指标、SBOM依赖分析和KEV匹配等评估发现：治理与继承的依赖风险无关；农粮项目原始Scorecard得分低，但经规模和成熟度匹配后差距不再显著。结论是确保该生态系统安全应投资于贡献者能力和依赖卫生，而非农业特有的控制手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业软件生态依赖大量开源组件，形成一个领域特定的软件供应链，但学术界对其供应链安全状况关注甚少。研究者希望弄清该生态的安全态势是否与同类非农业软件不同，以及这种差异是源于农业领域本身还是源于项目规模与成熟度。

Method: 构建AgOSS数据集，涵盖6个架构类别共66个仓库；采用OpenSSF Scorecard进行供应链安全评分，用治理指标衡量社区活动，基于SBOM进行依赖分析，并匹配已知被利用漏洞（KEV）；同时对非农业软件进行匹配作为对照组，通过回归和匹配分析领域效应与混淆变量。

Result: 两个关键发现：(1)治理水平与继承依赖风险大体独立——Scorecard反映社区活跃度，但与漏洞数量、漏洞密度或已知被利用漏洞数量均无关联，回归显示风险暴露信号落在架构类别而非领域上；(2)农粮项目原始Scorecard得分远低，但项目规模和成熟度是混淆因素，经匹配和回归后该差距不再显著。

Conclusion: 农粮开源生态的安全差距并非源于农业领域本身的独特风险，而主要归因于项目规模与成熟度不足。因此，提升该生态的供应链安全应聚焦于增强贡献者能力和改善依赖卫生，而非制定农业特有的安全控制措施。

Abstract: Much of agriculture depends on open-source software spanning farm management platforms, cloud services, edge gateways, embedded systems, and field-deployed sensors, forming a domain-specific software supply chain that has drawn little empirical security attention. It is unknown whether this ecosystem's supply chain security posture differs from that of comparable non-agricultural software, and if it does, whether the difference reflects the agricultural domain or the size and maturity of the projects within it.
  As a step towards securing agricultural open-source software, we present AgOSS, a dataset of 66 repositories across six architectural categories. We assess supply chain security within the dataset via OpenSSF Scorecard, governance metrics, SBOM-based dependency analysis, and KEV matching, and compare against matched non-agricultural controls. We report two findings. First, governance is largely independent of inherited dependency risk. Scorecard tracks community activity, but we detect no association with vulnerability count, density, or known-exploited count, and in regression the exposure signal loads on architectural category rather than domain. Second, agricultural projects score far lower on raw Scorecard, but size and maturity confound the gap: the residual loses significance under matching and regression. Securing this ecosystem means investing in contributor capacity and dependency hygiene, not agriculture-specific controls.

</details>
